Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


### [149] [OSReward: Instituting Standardized Evaluation for Cross-Platform Computer-Use Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28609)
*Qiushi Sun,Kanzhi Cheng,Yian Wang,Bowen Yang,Hang Yan,Liheng Chen,Fangzhi Xu,Zichen Ding,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Xingdong Gong,Zehao Li,Kaiming Jin,Xinfeng Yuan,Zhoumianze Liu,Jingyang Gong,Zhangyue Yin,Jiahui Gao,Zhiyong Wu,Tianbao Xie,Jianbing Zhang,Ben Kao,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出OSReward基准，系统评估VLM作为CUA轨迹评判者的可靠性，发现现有模型存在系统性宽松偏差，并构建OS-Shepherd-100K数据集训练开源奖励模型OS-Shepherd，以低成本提供可靠奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA评估依赖VLM评判轨迹，但VLM评判者的可靠性未被系统检验，且人工验证无法规模化，需要可靠的自动化评判方案。

Method: 构建OSReward基准（含多平台真实轨迹、人工标注真实裁决），衍生OSReward-Hard和OSReward-Multi；收集OS-Shepherd-100K推理标注语料，训练OS-Shepherd（9B/35B）开源奖励模型。

Result: 最先进VLM均未达到理想评判者水平，存在系统性宽松偏差（将失败误判为成功）；开源模型性能落后，但OS-Shepherd能以30-60%更低成本匹配商业评判者。

Conclusion: VLM评判可靠性需专门优化，OS-Shepherd提供低成本可靠的替代方案，相关分析为规模化可靠CUA奖励设计提供指导。

Abstract: Computer-using agents (CUAs) are advancing rapidly across the digital world. A CUA trajectory records the agent's actions, states, and reasoning. Verifying whether it fulfilled the task instruction is central to CUA evaluation, data curation, and reinforcement learning. Neither human-written verifiers nor human annotators can provide such verification at scale, so the field increasingly turns to vision-language models (VLMs) as judges of CUA trajectories. But a fundamental question has long gone unexamined: are these VLM judges reliable enough? To study it systematically, we introduce OSReward, a realistic, high-quality benchmark that evaluates VLM judges on CUA trajectories. The trajectories come from diverse agent backbones executing human-verified instructions across platforms, then rigorously labeled with ground-truth verdicts through multi-stage human annotation. Building on it, we derive OSReward-Hard, a challenge set concentrating genuinely hard cases, and OSReward-Multi for fine-grained efficiency and alignment scoring. The most comprehensive evaluation of VLM judges to date finds even state-of-the-art models fall short of an ideal judge, sharing a systematic leniency bias that mislabels failed runs as successes. The few reliable enough to trust are too expensive to run at scale, while affordable open models trail far behind. To close this gap, we construct and release OS-Shepherd-100K, an open corpus of reasoning-annotated trajectory judgments for the CUA community. On it, we train OS-Shepherd (9B and 35B), open reward models that supply low-cost, stable, and reliable reward signals, matching commercial judges at 30-60% lower cost than the frontier. Extensive analyses further inform the design of reliable CUA reward at scale. Our code, benchmark, dataset, and model checkpoints are available at https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/.

</details>


### [150] [AISPA: User-Centric System Prompt Auditing for Large Language Model Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28617)
*Xiangning Lin,Shenzhe Zhu,Shu Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Haoqian Zhang,Yipeng Zhao,Chengxuan Qian,Tianwei Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Zhenlong Yuan,Dingcheng Wang,Juncheng Wu,Yuan Si,Jiaxin Liu,Baolong Bi,Robert Mahari,Tobin South,Dazza Greenwood,Zexue He,Rishi Bommasani,Sophia Kazinnik,Andreas Haupt,Samuele Marro,Erik Brynjolfsson,Alex Pentland,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AISPA框架，审计88个商业AI产品的3,249条系统提示指令，发现防护指令普遍但浅层、问题指令仍普遍，呼吁提高透明度和监管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示词在商业AI中广泛使用但不公开，导致信任和责任差距，缺乏用户视角的系统性审计框架。

Method: 构建AISPA用户中心审计框架，沿八个维度评估系统提示词，对88个商业产品的系统提示指令进行分类（防护性或问题性），并进行统计分析。

Result: 发现系统提示设计差异大（防护指令数量从<5到>60）；98.9%产品有防护指令但仅24%覆盖全部维度；提示词随时间变长且更防护；约40%产品含至少一条不利于用户的指令。

Conclusion: 商业AI系统提示词需更大透明度、标准化和独立监督，现有进展不足以弥合用户保护缺口。

Abstract: System prompts are instructions configured by developers to govern the behaviors of foundation models in AI applications. They are used throughout commercial AI products, but are rarely disclosed to the public or regulators, creating a serious trust and accountability gap in the wide deployment of AI systems. In this paper, we introduce Artificial Intelligence System Prompt Assurance (AISPA), a user-centric framework for systematically auditing system prompts in AI systems. AISPA examines specific parts of a system prompt and evaluates them along eight dimensions that matter to users. We then use this framework to review 3,249 instructions from system prompts in 88 commercial AI products, classifying each instruction as either protective (of users) or problematic. Our audit surfaces four core findings. First, system prompt design varies substantially across products and developers, with some organizations averaging over 60 protective instructions per product while others average fewer than 5. Second, protective instructions are widely adopted but shallow in scope: 98.9% of products contain at least one, yet only 24% cover all eight dimensions of the AISPA taxonomy. Third, system prompts have grown steadily longer and more protective of users, suggesting that user protection is becoming a more visible concern in commercial prompt design. Fourth, despite this progress, problematic instructions remain pervasive: roughly 40% of products contain at least one instruction that works against user interests, and protective and problematic instructions frequently coexist within the same prompt. Our findings highlight the need for greater transparency, standardization, and independent oversight for system prompts in commercial AI products.

</details>


<div id='Training'></div>

# Training [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出RLPF方法，通过分阶段奖励训练代码智能体优化程序效率，使正确率从11.1%提升至54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升至38.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型训练仅关注正确性，忽略运行效率，导致通过相同测试的程序性能差异大，而运行时作为奖励信号脆弱且难以直接使用。

Method: RLPF将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励：失败程序按执行进度排序，正确程序按相对基线到专家参考的改进程度排序，从而在正确性前后分别提供有用和性能敏感的反馈。

Result: 在PerfCodeBench上微调Qwen3-32B，正确且可运行解从11.1%升至54.6%，相对效率从8.1%升至38.6%，并超越更强开源模型，且迁移至EffiBench-X表现良好。

Conclusion: 代码智能体可通过性能反馈训练，不仅通过测试，还能主动优化程序效率；模型生成参考虽有效但较弱，组合奖励优于仅正确性或仅运行时基线。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [152] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出SDO框架，通过暴露驱动反馈机制动态组织数据批次，在SFT/DPO/GRPO中加速收敛并提升准确率均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练效率优化忽视数据组织的静态性，固定分组无法适应训练中样本暴露变化，导致冗余更新与欠优化。

Method: SDO基于冻结外部嵌入，按epoch用KNN邻域遍历形成局部一致批次，并跨epoch记录样本参与次数以降低过暴露样本采样概率。

Result: 在SFT、DPO、GRPO中加速收敛，尤其早中期增益最大，产生更连贯梯度且各题型准确率更均衡，未永久排除样本。

Conclusion: SDO作为即插即用框架，无需模型热身开销即可提升后训练效率与效果平衡性。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [153] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源Python库，统一加载、协调和增强放射学数据集，支持胸片、CT和MRI，标准化24个公共数据集，提供深度学习就绪的数据交付、多种标注类型和交互式可视化，并引入AI代理技能简化新数据集集成，通过参考基线RadHarmony-ViT验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练放射学图像的深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、文件格式、目录布局、标签方案和标注类型的异构数据集，现有工具缺乏统一接口，导致数据预处理和集成成本高、重复劳动多。

Method: RadHarmony提供统一API，标准化元数据为表格格式，封装MONAI数据集用于深度学习采样和磁盘缓存，支持分类标签、分割掩膜、边界框和报告文本，并开发AI代理技能自动引导数据集成流程（从原始数据检查到代码生成和测试）。

Result: 通过结合三个异构胸片数据集预训练RadHarmony-ViT视觉Transformer基线，无需数据集特定代码，证明了库的实用性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: RadHarmony降低了多源放射学数据集的整合与使用门槛，促进深度学习模型开发，代码和预训练权重公开可用，便于社区采用和扩展。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [154] [Distributed Point Functions and Function Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27696)
*Elette Boyle,Niv Gilboa,Yuval Ishai,Peter Scholl*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文综述了分布式点函数（DPF）的定义、构造与应用，并探讨其向函数秘密共享（FSS）的推广及影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPF结构轻量，支撑多种应用（如私有信息检索、匿名消息、预计算安全计算等），但缺乏系统性梳理，需厘清其原理与拓展方向。

Method: 通过调研整理DPF的定义、构造方案，并分析其推广至FSS的机制及效率意义。

Result: 系统总结了DPF的核心构造与典型应用，并指出高效FSS方案可类似推广多数DPF应用场景。

Conclusion: DPF是重要基础原语，其向FSS的扩展能覆盖更广泛函数类，从而提升相关应用的通用性与效率。

Abstract: A distributed point function (DPF) is a cryptographic primitive that enables compressed additive sharing of a secret weight-1 vector (equivalently, a point function) across two or more parties. The appealing lightweight structure of DPF constructions has enabled a wide range of applications. These include private information retrieval, anonymous messaging, secure computation with preprocessing, and pseudorandom correlation generators for expanding small correlated seeds into large pseudorandom instances of cryptographic correlations.
  In this article, we survey definitions, constructions, and applications of DPFs. We also discuss the extension of DPF to function secret sharing (FSS), which generalizes point functions to support richer function classes. Efficient FSS schemes yield a similar generalization for most of the applications of DPFs.

</details>


### [155] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 4比特权重量化在标准指标上看似无损，但通过更细粒度的诊断发现，量化会放大模型已有的工具名幻觉等失败，幅度可达2.5倍，只是被基准的十错误预算掩盖；将预算缩至2个错误后，分数差距重新暴露（17分），且可通过针对性修复提示消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究广泛声称4比特后训练量化几乎无损，但这类结论多基于单轮或简单任务；多轮工具调用智能体是当前最关键的应用场景，需要严格检验量化在标准指标和过程行为上的真实影响。

Method: 在τ²-bench上对两种开源模型家族（稠密与MoE变体）的八个单元进行16、8、4比特量化对比，共456个episode每单元；除标准评分外，新增两个诊断：逐通道错误率分析和缩小错误预算（从10到2）的鲁棒性测试，并结合等价检验和针对工具名幻觉的修复提示验证因果机制。

Result: 标准指标下无单元通过多重比较校正，最大过程损伤单元等价检验限定变化在±7.5分内；但量化将工具名幻觉失败量放大至2.5倍（每任务+17.6分），失败类型集与全精度高度一致（秩相关≥0.94，0.18%新事件）；缩小错误预算到2后，仅在量化增大错误量的单元出现17分差距，修复提示可精确消除该损伤。

Conclusion: 量化损失被基准的容错设计掩盖而非真正不存在；建议在任务奖励之外，报告逐通道错误率和缩小预算下的成功率作为标准诊断，以更真实反映量化对关键失败模式的影响。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [156] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 语言模型能力的形成受制于多重电路成分的随机联合对齐，这一‘无部分积分’的联合对齐是能力涌现的限速步骤；通过速率方程、预注册实验和干预手段，验证了该机制并提出了修复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未解释为何能力出现是突变性的，且标准监控指标（如验证损失）无法预测或察觉能力涌现前的关键阶段；本文旨在揭示能力形成的核心瓶颈，并提供可预测、可干预的机制。

Method: 提出两指纹（缺件等待时间与消融后残存能力）证明联合对齐是关键；构建速率方程（位点×尝试×驱动×exp(-βK)-破坏）并预注册冻结常数；在前向、后向及干预实验中验证，包括关键/值切片重初始化、门控注意力模型证明及SGD噪声的涨落耗散检验。

Result: 缺件数而非电路大小决定等待时间（1.19-1.37）；消融单部分使能力残存中位数17%（预测50-83%，p=2e-10）；前向可精确预测爆发步骤（10/10通过，0/12低于阈值），后向显示延迟学习导致永久失效而验证损失平滑；重初始化查询-关键切片恢复可学习性（6/6），值切片无效（0/6）。

Conclusion: 能力形成的关键在于电路各成分的随机联合对齐，其失败是不可部分补偿的；修复机制（重初始化QK切片）有效，且噪声性质可被校正以按计划调控电路形成，适用范围为1.4B以内的Transformer中的合取电路。

Abstract: A capability appears in a language model when the last parts of its circuit align in one stochastic attempt, and getting all but one right is worth nothing. We show this no-partial-credit joint alignment is the rate-limiting step of capability formation. Two fingerprints: in a shortcut-free apparatus a five-part circuit missing three waits as long as a three-part circuit missing three (1.19-1.37), so the wait counts missing parts, not size; and on Pythia across seven capabilities and three scales, ablating one part leaves a median 17% of the capability in 32 of 32 discriminating cells, where partial credit predicts 50-83% (p = 2e-10), while a random non-part head leaves 100%.
  One rare event whose barrier grows with missing parts yields a rate equation -- sites x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K), minus destruction -- read three ways, each preregistered with frozen constants. Forward: a capability flat at baseline ignites at a step of our choosing once the mix passes a concentration floor (10/10 above, 0/12 below), and while still flat its arrival is datable from its precursor to 5% median error on six held-out models. Backward: the delay to learn a withheld capability grows with waiting until, past a critical step, it never ignites -- yet validation loss falls smoothly throughout, so standard monitors are blind to it. We locate the damage (heads commit to the base data) and isolate the cure: re-initializing only the query-key slices restores learnability (6/6) while the value slices do nothing (0/6). We prove the mechanism in a controlled gated-attention model: occupation forces a deadline whose consequences need no mixing assumption. Completed: SGD's noise fails the fluctuation-dissipation test, so we install one and anneal, melt and pin circuits on schedule. Scope: conjunction circuits in transformers to 1.4B.

</details>


### [157] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMentor，将盲点诊断作为独立于执行的可学习能力，通过强化学习训练Mentor策略生成诊断任务、识别失败模式并整理为纠正技能，在不更新执行器权重或依赖人工标注下，平均提升执行器性能44.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我进化主要关注学习如何行动，忽视学习发现自身未知的能力；同时已有方法假设失败发现是给定的，仅关注识别后的修复。作者提出疑问：盲点诊断本身是否可学习，并为此排除执行器适应和人类监督两种替代进步来源，强制所有改进源于诊断能力。

Method: 提出SkillMentor框架，训练一个Mentor策略（通过强化学习）来生成诊断任务、识别重复失败模式，并将这些模式整理成可复用的纠正技能；执行器本身权重不更新，且无人工标注示例。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准上，SkillMentor平均提升执行器性能44.2%，证明盲点诊断是可学习的。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断是可学习的能力，使智能体无需更新执行器权重或依赖人工筛选数据即可实现自我进化，为自我进化研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [158] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，通过图神经网络建模历史轨迹并预判动作错误，使LLM智能体在长时程任务中平均通过率提升14.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程交互任务中常因单步次优动作导致累积错误，耗尽步数预算；现有方法依赖昂贵微调，缺乏低成本、可泛化的错误预判机制。

Method: 将历史轨迹建模为概率图，利用图神经网络识别频繁导致失败的序列动作模式；在动作执行前作为诊断沙箱提供早期预警，促使智能体自我修正。

Result: 在四个基准、三个LLM智能体上平均pass ratio提升14.69%。

Conclusion: 通过前置错误诊断可有效缓解长时程任务的复合误差，为免微调增强LLM智能体提供可行范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully. The extensive experiments on four benchmarks with three LLM agents demonstrate a $14.69\%$ pass ratio improvement on average.

</details>


### [159] [MedLLM: An Open Medical Language Model at the Sub-Billion Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27490)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Asim Ahmed,Mihan Mohagheghzadeh,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出MedLLM，一个0.1B参数的开放医疗语言模型，通过三阶段训练（通用预训练、域微调、偏好对齐）在下十亿参数规模下发现医疗能力随压缩呈现任务类型分化的模式，而非均匀退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放医疗语言模型均停留在7B或以上参数规模，缺乏对低于十亿参数子区间的系统表征；0.1B模型可能揭示容量稀缺时医疗能力如何选择性保留或丧失。

Method: 使用完全开放的三阶段流程：通用预训练采用课程式序列长度调度；域微调基于MedFineWeb语料库（通过嵌入相似性从通用网页数据中筛选与医疗问答相关的参考引导语料）；偏好对齐结合监督微调与直接偏好优化。

Result: 在上下文基础问答中，MedLLM仅比医疗适配7B模型低2.9个百分点，并超越指令微调及通用7B基线；在知识回忆问答中，MedQA接近任务底线，但MedMCQA显著优于所有7B及以下基线，表明7B下能力共存的分化在低容量时显现。

Conclusion: 医疗能力在低容量下按任务类型非均匀退化，知识回忆受限源于容量而非适配；该分离现象仅在容量匮乏时暴露，对小型医疗模型部署有指导意义。

Abstract: Open medical language models have converged on a single scale: every widely used system runs at 7B parameters or more, leaving the sub-billion regime uncharacterized. We present MedLLM, an open 0.1B-parameter medical language model trained through a fully open three-phase pipeline: general pretraining with curriculum sequence-length scheduling, domain fine-tuning on MedFineWeb, a reference-guided medical corpus we release that is selected from general web data by embedding similarity to medical question-answering (QA) data, and preference-aligned fine-tuning combining SFT with direct preference optimization (DPO). Across medical benchmarks, MedLLM shows a pattern visible only at sub-billion scale: medical competence does not degrade uniformly under compression but splits by task type. On context-grounded QA it comes within $2.9$pp of a medically adapted 7B model and surpasses the instruction-tuned and general-purpose 7B baselines; on knowledge-recall QA it stays near the task floor on clinical-vignette MedQA yet significantly exceeds every 7B and sub-7B baseline on MedMCQA, indicating that where recall fails the constraint is model capacity rather than adaptation. This dissociation is masked at 7B, where both capabilities are present, and surfaces only when capacity is scarce.

</details>


### [160] [AI Literacy: An Exercise in Power-Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27547)
*Brady D. Lund,Zoë Abbie Teel*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文批判现有AI素养框架的“消费者”导向，基于福柯、弗莱雷和数字素养研究，提出将AI素养重构为批判实践，培养个体批判性评估、抵制和参与治理AI的认知主体性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已成为社会重要知识生产系统，但现有AI素养框架仅强调技术能力和负责任使用，强化了“消费者”取向，忽视了认知主体性，且未解决AI工具获取不平等导致的认知不公正。

Method: 采用理论分析，整合福柯的权力-知识、弗莱雷的批判意识教育学及数字素养研究，提出AI素养的三部分框架：情境化使用、批判性追问、参与式治理。

Result: 提出了一个以赋权为导向的AI素养重构框架，将素养目标从“胜任的消费者”转向“认知主体”，强调批判性评估、结构抵抗和治理参与。

Conclusion: AI素养应被理解为培养认知主体的批判实践，必须纳入结构性不平等考量，以促进个体在AI系统中的能动性和民主参与。

Abstract: As generative artificial intelligence becomes one of the most significant systems of knowledge production in our society today, questions relating to who can access and shape that production grow increasingly important in our discourse. This paper argues that the existing frameworks for AI literacy, which are dominated by technical competency and responsible-use principles, are insufficient because they enforce a "consumer" orientation toward AI rather than fostering genuine epistemic agency. Based upon Foucault's concept of power-knowledge, Freire's pedagogy of critical consciousness, and scholarship of digital literacy, this paper proposes a reconceptualization of AI literacy as a critical practice that equips individuals not just to use AI systems, but to critically evaluate them, resist their structuring assumptions, and participate in their governance. The paper further argues that unequal access to AI tools in society recapitulates longstanding epistemic injustices, and that a literacy framework oriented toward empowerment must account for these structural inequities. A three-part framework of AI literacy based on the notions of contextual use, critical interrogation, and participatory governance frames this literacy as a cultivation of epistemic "agents" rather than the training of competent consumers of AI-generated information.

</details>


### [161] [Latent States in Neural Networks: Recovering the Temporal Structure of Drifting Data from Model Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27482)
*Kevin Guan*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 通过隐马尔可夫模型拟合时序模型权重，可恢复数据流中的离散阶段（regimes），且这些状态对跨窗口迁移有增益，超越时间邻近性和等分基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序漂移可能呈现离散阶段而非连续变化，但现有方法未验证是否可从模型权重中恢复这些阶段，并评估其对泛化的价值。

Method: 在Fakeddit（多模态虚假信息）和Yelp（情感分析）上，按时间窗训练分类器，对齐权重后拟合HMM，提取隐状态划分时间线，并检验同状态窗口间的迁移优势，控制时间邻近、等分分割、类别分布漂移及权重空间几何。

Result: 两个数据集上同状态迁移优势显著，且优于等分基线；状态与类别分布变化相关强于权重几何；残差化后优势仍超过置换零分布，但Yelp上效应减弱。

Conclusion: 模型权重轨迹中的HMM状态能捕获数据流中与迁移相关的离散结构，且独立于标签分布漂移，具有一定通用性，但稳定性数据上效应较弱。

Abstract: A temporally drifting data stream may pass through discrete regimes rather than changing continuously. We ask whether such regimes are recoverable from the weights of models trained on the stream, using a hidden Markov model (HMM) fit to the chronologically ordered trajectory of those weights. We study this question in two domains known to drift over time: multimodal misinformation detection, using the Fakeddit dataset; and sentiment analysis, using the Yelp dataset. We train classifiers on consecutive temporal windows and fit an HMM to the trajectory of their aligned weights, recovering latent states that partition each timeline into coherent phases. On both datasets, classifiers generalize better to data from windows sharing the state of their training window than to windows across state boundaries. This within-state transfer advantage survives a control for temporal proximity and modestly exceeds the advantage recovered by a naive partition into contiguous states of equal size. Although the states are estimated solely from model weights, they correlate more strongly with shifts in the data's class distribution than with the weight-space geometry used to estimate them. After class divergence and lag are residualized out, the within-state advantage exceeds its permutation null on both tasks, indicating that the states recover structure relevant to transfer beyond the data distribution. Every effect replicates on both tasks but is attenuated on Yelp, whose label distribution is more temporally stable.

</details>


### [162] [Hidden APIs in Language Models: Discovering Reusable Causal Interfaces from Forked Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27617)
*SiYuan Ma,Yiqin Luo,Zhangji,Canran Xiao,Albert Gao,Wei-Hsing Huang,Wei Wang,Qiwei Wu,Xinran Li,Jinfeng Wei,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需人工标注潜在标签的因果商方法（forked futures），通过比较隐藏状态在未来操作下的响应分布，评估语言模型内部可复用接口的存在性。在多个模型上，Shared接口的预测编码长度最短，因果路径中介效应显著，且架构识别准确率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探针方法仅基于当前答案的相似性，无法证明隐藏状态支持可复用的未来计算，从而缺乏对内部接口的因果性验证。

Method: 引入forked futures策略，在状态形成后采样未来操作并比较诱导的响应分布；定义Shared、Local、Mixture、Distributed四种接口，通过预拟因果描述长度、未来签名保真度和容量约束进行竞争选择，并辅以移植分析和路径中介检验。

Result: Shared接口在Qwen2.5-1.5B上降低0.216 nats、在Llama-3-8B上降低0.294 nats的保持外描述长度；未来签名失真紧密；移植分析中Shared在正确性、局部性、复制保留和复合指标上最强；路径中介效应为0.749 vs 0.150；16个架构中正确恢复14个。

Conclusion: 在测试的操作库、干预和保持未来条件下，Shared接口作为经济可复用的因果接口得到支持，但结论明确受限于候选架构和实验设定。

Abstract: Identical language-model answers can arise from hidden states that support different future computations, so current-answer probes do not establish a reusable internal interface. We introduce forked futures: future operations are sampled only after a prefix state has formed, and states are compared through the response distributions induced by those operations. This yields an empirical causal quotient over hidden states without requiring researcher-specified latent labels. Shared, Local, Mixture, and Distributed interfaces then compete under prequential causal description length subject to future-signature fidelity and matched capacity constraints. In the two detailed model evaluations, Shared has the lowest held-out description length, with gains of 0.216 nats on Qwen2.5-1.5B and 0.294 nats on Llama-3-8B, while maintaining tightly clustered mean future-signature distortion; a five-backbone sweep preserves the positive direction of Sharedness Gain. The figure-aligned transplantation analysis gives Shared the strongest joint target-correctness, locality, copy-preservation, and composite profile, and API-aligned paths mediate 0.749 of the target effect versus 0.150 for matched null paths. In the blind four-class model-organism test, 14/16 architectures are recovered, with one observed non-Shared to Shared error among 12 non-Shared organisms. These results support an economical reusable causal interface within the tested operation banks, while keeping the claim explicitly conditional on the candidate architectures, interventions, and held-out futures.

</details>


### [163] [When Does Explicit View Routing Work? A Controlled Study of Multi-View Graph-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27530)
*Xiao Yue,Guangzhi Qu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验检验多视图图-文本检索中的语义路由，发现显式外部标签和属性路由有效，但未证实自由形式路由或一致的多视图专业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图-文本检索常将图和描述映射为单一嵌入，多头部虽能分离语义，但检索可能依赖架构通道而非内容语义，需厘清条件。

Method: 采用MV-GTA受控版本：确定性文本段、隔离编码器、视图特定图头，基于外部标签或RDKit描述符定义相关性，通过正确路由与随机置换对比进行因果测试。

Result: BBBP和BACE上正确路由提升nDCG 0.305-0.685；期望图头优于错误头0.303-0.453；联合模型在拓扑/标签/属性nDCG达0.720/1.000/0.877，优于专家模型；属性释义增强提升未见模板nDCG 0.140-0.147，但一致性和硬模板扩展有时降低性能。

Conclusion: 证据仅支持显式外部标签和属性路由及多界面整合，不确立自由形式路由、一致三视图专业化或统计等价于专家模型，也无下游预测优越性。

Abstract: Graph-text retrieval typically maps a graph and its description to a single embedding, even when a query concerns only one semantic aspect, such as a class label or molecular property. Multiple heads can separate these aspects, but a change in the query head may alter retrieval even when the wrong text is sent to that head. Such behavior demonstrates architectural channelization, not necessarily semantic routing. We examine the conditions under which this distinction can be resolved. Our controlled version of MV-GTA uses deterministic, verifiable text segments; isolated text encoders; view-specific graph heads; and relevance derived from external labels or RDKit descriptors. Correct routing and per-sample derangements form a causal test of whether retrieval depends on content. On BBBP and BACE, correct routing improves label and property nDCG by 0.305 to 0.685 over deranged training. The expected graph head exceeds the best wrong head by 0.303 to 0.453. Topology does not specialize consistently across the two datasets. In a matched three-seed comparison, one joint model obtains mean topology, label, and property nDCG of 0.720/1.000/0.877; three separately trained Single specialists obtain 0.633/0.976/0.859. Property paraphrase augmentation also improves unseen-template nDCG by 0.140 and 0.147 over a matched-exposure canonical control. Consistency and hard-template extensions, however, reduce canonical retrieval in some settings. The evidence is therefore limited to explicit, externally grounded label and property routing and observed multi-interface consolidation. It does not establish free-form routing, consistent three-view specialization, statistical equivalence to specialists, or superior downstream prediction.

</details>


### [164] [HealthCAT: An Interpretable Encoder-only Transformer Framework for Health Indicator Prediction and Temporal Interpretation of Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27635)
*Xiaotong Yu,Joshua Y. Kim,HaeJin Lee,Kalina Yacef*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: HealthCAT是一个结合编码器Transformer与注意力类激活令牌的框架，用于可穿戴传感器数据的分类和逐时间步解释，在真实数据上显著提升性能且解释具有预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在可穿戴传感器健康分析中优先预测精度而忽略可解释性，限制其在健康研究中的应用，需要既能准确预测又能提供时间级行为模式解释的模型。

Method: 采用Encoder-only Transformer架构，引入Attentive Class Activation Token生成类别特定、时间步级别的解释，将解释映射回行为周期（如昼夜节律）以支持个体层面分析。

Result: 在两组真实数据集（共306人）上，F1分数提升高达17%，准确率提升12%；掩蔽实验显示识别的时间步比随机选择携带更多预测信息（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: HealthCAT通过结合预测性能与验证的时间步解释，推动可穿戴数据分析从聚合指标转向时间模式，有助于健康监测、行为分析和干预设计。

Abstract: Wearable sensors continuously capture fine-grained multivariate time-series data, providing opportunities to model behavioural patterns associated with health outcomes. However, existing deep learning methods prioritise predictive accuracy over interpretability, limiting their application in health research. In this study, we present HealthCAT, a flexible framework that integrates an Encoder-only Transformer with an Attentive Class Activation Token (AttentiveCAT) to generate class-specific, time-step-level interpretations. These interpretations can be mapped back onto behavioural cycles that are relevant to the domain (e.g., time-of-day), supporting individual-level analysis of wearable sensor data. We evaluated HealthCAT using two real-world wearable sensor datasets (306 participants in total). HealthCAT outperformed deep learning baselines by up to 17\% in F1-score and 12\% in accuracy on both datasets ($p<0.05$). In masking experiments, the time steps identified by HealthCAT carried significantly more predictive value than random selection across all masking conditions ($p<0.05$), indicating that the identified time steps are predictively informative. By coupling predictive performance with validated time-step-level interpretability, HealthCAT moves wearable sensor analysis beyond aggregated metrics towards temporal patterns that support health monitoring, behavioural pattern analysis, and intervention design in health research. The significance of this work is that it enables accurate prediction of health indicators from wearable sensor data while providing insights into when and how physical activity patterns occur, rather than relying solely on aggregated summary measures.

</details>


### [165] [HALO: Heterogeneous Admission through Localized Obligations for Safe Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27636)
*Taewoo Park,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Kiseok Kim,Seunghyun Yoo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: HALO是一种运行时协议，用于在动态变化的环境中保留智能体响应中仍受支持的组件，同时独立检查各动作并在必要时替换受阻动作，实验显示其优于整体拒绝策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统返回的异构响应可能包含多个组件，但环境条件可能在外部使用前变化，导致部分组件失效。整体拒绝会浪费仍有效的组件，而独立检查则可能使依赖组件失去前提。

Method: 提出HALO协议，通过声明前提与局部义务机制，保留前提仍受支持的组件，在派发前重新检查每个精确动作，并仅允许用新的候选动作替换被阻止的动作。

Result: HALO匹配全部96项准入预期并通过20项协议测试，在结构化回放中保留248/248个受支持组件（包括128/128个不受无关变化影响的组件），而整体策略仅保留0/248；在PX4/Gazebo冷启动测试中阻止了所有过时路线，完成所有新鲜恢复。

Conclusion: HALO有效解决了异构组件在环境变化下的选择性准入问题，避免了整包拒绝的浪费，同时保证了依赖完整性和动作时效性，在仿真中验证了其可靠性。

Abstract: Recent agentic AI systems may return a heterogeneous response containing notices, requests, handoffs, and actions. Conditions can change before external use, so components from the same response need not remain supported together. Rejecting the whole response discards useful components, whereas checking components independently can leave a dependent without its prerequisite. We present Heterogeneous Admission with Localized Obligations (HALO), a runtime protocol that preserves supported components whose declared prerequisites also remain supported, rechecks each exact action before dispatch, and allows blocked actions to be replaced only by fresh candidates. HALO matched all 96 admission expectations and passed all 20 protocol tests. In structured-response replay, it retained 248/248 supported components, including 128/128 unaffected by unrelated changes, while a whole-response policy retained 0/248. Across ten cold-start PX4/Gazebo sessions, HALO blocked every tested stale route, observed no matching stale setpoint, and completed all fresh recoveries.

</details>


### [166] [A Montage-Agnostic Encoder for Calibration-Light Cross-User Gesture Recognition from Surface Electromyography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27565)
*Jethro Odeyemi,W. J. Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种与电极布局无关的编码器，通过共享权重和物理坐标读取肌电信号，实现跨用户手势识别，在多数数据集上优于传统基线，但优势受基线强度和训练预算影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式识别控制的假肢手势识别器训练后难以跨用户迁移，通常需要新用户重新校准，限制了实际应用。

Method: 设计蒙太奇无关编码器：每个电极用共享权重读取，按物理坐标而非索引定位，使单一架构适应任意通道数；跨用户训练，并通过消融研究验证三个关键组件的作用。

Result: 在DB1上超过每用户Hudgins和线性判别分类器0.234宏F1，DB2上超过0.108，DB5上低于基线；消融显示每个关键组件单独贡献超过3-shot宏F1的一半；训练用户数从9到39变化时性能接近恒定；自监督预训练无额外收益。

Conclusion: 跨用户训练收益取决于每用户基线强度（由信号保真度决定），训练池大小仅作为收敛的稳定性下限；对比LDA基线需考虑训练预算和基线质量，自监督预训练在充分监督训练后无帮助。

Abstract: Pattern-recognition control promises a myoelectric prosthesis that responds to many intended gestures rather than one or two, but the promise has stayed in the laboratory. A recogniser trained on one person rarely transfers to the next, and useful performance usually demands a fresh round of labelled calibration from the end user. A montage-agnostic encoder is introduced that reads each electrode with shared weights and locates it by its physical coordinate rather than its index, so one architecture ingests any channel count without montage-specific parameters. Trained across users, it exceeds a per-user Hudgins and linear-discriminant classifier by 0.234 macro-F1 on DB1 for every held-out subject and by 0.108 on DB2, and falls below it on the ten-subject DB5. Each of the encoder's three key components individually accounts for more than half of its 3-shot macro F1 in an otherwise budget-matched ablation study. A controlled subject-count sweep shows the margin is close to flat from nine training subjects to thirty-nine, so the training pool binds only as a stability floor below which cross-user training fails to converge; what tracks the direction of the comparison across the three databases is instead the strength of the per-user baseline, which signal fidelity sets. Comparing against an LDA baseline depends on budget spent training models and on how good that baseline is, and self-supervised pretraining had no benefits once a supervised model was adequately trained.

</details>


### [167] [Evaluating and Pricing Advertisements in AI-Generated Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27686)
*John L. Turner-Smith,Zimeng Huang,Yuhan Fu,Yihang Zhang,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于心理学启发的智能体模拟框架，生成监督信号，并蒸馏出参数高效的评估器，用于预测点击意图及广告质量三维度，进而支持定价和广告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的搜索引擎中广告内嵌于生成回复，需兼顾用户效用和商业价值，但点击意图缺乏行为日志、人工标注难以校准、前沿LLM评判混淆意图与语言流畅性，导致定价和信号生成陷入循环困境。

Method: 通过心理学启发的智能体模拟生成监督信号，蒸馏为参数高效评估器，输出平滑可微的点击意图和广告质量估计；基于该信号推导唯一支付规则，实现真实报价最优，并扩展至非单调分配。

Result: 评估器在相关性敏感度上达79%（对比前沿零样本评判的60-67%），能跟踪分级内容退化，对103个虚构产品零错误泛化，与五名标注者的偏好一致性达86%，且一致性随置信度上升。

Conclusion: 该可微信号可直接用于定价机制和广告生成训练目标，为LLM驱动的广告系统提供统一且原理化的解决方案。

Abstract: As search increasingly shifts toward LLM-driven answer engines, advertising is becoming embedded within the generated response itself and should therefore be evaluated for both user utility and commercial value. The key challenge is click-through intent: behavioural logs are unavailable, human annotation resists calibration, and frontier LLM judges conflate intent with linguistic fluency. These gaps compound, as principled pricing presupposes a continuous intent signal, while generating such a signal presupposes supervision that is currently unavailable. We construct the missing supervision through a psychologically grounded agent simulation framework, and distil it into a parameter-efficient evaluator that predicts click-through intent, together with the three companion dimensions of ad quality, as smooth, differentiable estimates. Validated through sign-certain behavioural perturbations, the evaluator surpasses frontier zero-shot judges on relevance sensitivity (79% versus 60-67%), tracks graded content degradation, generalises without error to 103 fictional products, and agrees with human preference in 86% of pairwise judgements across five annotators, with agreement rising in the evaluator's confidence. Upon its estimates we build the pricing layer directly, deriving the unique payment rule under which truthful bidding is optimal, demonstrating it on a best-of-k allocation, and extending the mechanism to non-monotone allocations. The same differentiable signal stands ready as a training objective for ad generation.

</details>


### [168] [Guiding Large Language Models with Genetic Programming-Evolved Heuristic Knowledge for Dynamic Multi-Mode Project Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27698)
*Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出从遗传规划（GP）进化出的高质量调度规则中提取知识，以四种方式（特征选择、特征提示、规则参考、规则跟随）引导零样本大语言模型（LLM）进行动态多模式项目调度决策，并评估其性能、令牌消耗、稳定性和理由特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本LLM决策缺乏领域知识、令牌消耗高且结果不稳定；而GP进化规则虽有效但需领域专家设计启发式。现有LLM-GP混合方法多用于支持进化，本文反向利用GP知识提升LLM在线决策质量。

Method: 从高质量GP规则中提取调度知识，通过特征选择、特征提示、规则参考和规则跟随四种注入机制指导LLM决策，并对比无引导LLM，评估调度性能、令牌数、决策稳定性及生成理由中的特征焦点。

Result: GP引导普遍优于无引导LLM，但表征方式关键：简化上下文或提供显式决策逻辑（特征选择、规则跟随）比突出特征（特征提示）更有效；特征选择令牌效率最高，规则跟随性能强但成本高；引导还提升决策稳定性并改变生成理由中的特征表达。

Conclusion: GP知识反向注入可有效提升LLM调度决策，但需匹配合适的表示方式以平衡性能与成本，未来应关注机制选择与自适应引导策略。

Abstract: In dynamic multi-mode project scheduling, activities have alternative execution modes and uncertain durations, while precedence relations and limited resources constrain their execution. Heuristic priority rules support fast online decisions, but their design requires substantial domain expertise. Genetic programming (GP) hyper-heuristics can automatically evolve such rules. Large language models (LLMs), meanwhile, provide a flexible interface for interpreting scheduling information and explaining decisions. However, zero-shot LLM decisions may lack domain knowledge, consume many tokens, and vary across repeated queries. GP-evolved rules therefore provide a potential source of scheduling knowledge for guiding LLM decisions. Unlike existing LLM--GP hybrids that use LLMs to support heuristic evolution, we transfer knowledge in the reverse direction, using knowledge extracted from high-quality GP rules to guide an online LLM decision maker. We extract knowledge from high-quality GP rules and inject it through Feature Selection, Feature Hint, Rule Reference, and Rule Follow. These mechanisms are evaluated in terms of scheduling performance, token consumption, decision stability, and the feature focus expressed in generated rationales. GP-derived guidance generally improves the unguided LLM, but its representation matters. Simplifying the decision context or supplying explicit decision logic is more effective than highlighting important features. Feature Selection offers the best token efficiency, whereas Rule Follow achieves strong performance at greater token cost. Guidance also improves decision stability and changes the features expressed in generated rationales.

</details>


### [169] [SpecCal: Ambiguity-Aware Candidate Calibration for Infrared Spectrum-Based Molecular Structure Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27788)
*Yixuan Chen,Bo Liu,Yusen Tan,Guokun Yang,Wenjie Du,Jun Xia*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出SpecCal，一种无需训练的候选校准框架，通过重排序和引入光谱一致的候选分子，提升红外光谱到分子结构的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外光谱结构信息有限，现有模型生成的候选分子集受生成偏好影响，可能未充分覆盖符合光谱约束的结构，导致重建结果存在歧义。

Method: SpecCal在现有基础模型输出上，通过光谱一致性指导重排序并补充结构合理的替代候选，无需参数更新，即插即用且模型无关。

Result: 在多个基准上，SpecCal一致提升不同基础模型在SMILES和骨架级别的top-k重建效果。

Conclusion: 在光谱歧义下校准候选集是提升分子重建性能的实用方法，代码已公开。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from infrared (IR) spectra is a fundamental yet challenging problem. A key difficulty is that an IR spectrum provides limited structural information: different molecules may share similar functional groups and local vibrational patterns, leading to highly similar spectral responses. Thus, even when an observed spectrum has a unique underlying structure, reconstructing it from the spectrum remains ambiguous. Existing IR-to-molecule models usually generate a ranked set of candidate molecules, but this set is largely determined by the model's learned generation preference and may not fully capture the structures that best satisfy the observed spectral constraints. To address this limitation, we propose SpecCal, a training-free candidate calibration framework for IR-to-molecule prediction. SpecCal operates on the candidate outputs of existing base models and improves the prediction set by re-ranking current candidates while introducing additional structurally plausible alternatives guided by spectral consistency. The framework is plug-and-play and model-agnostic, requiring no parameter updates for integration with diverse base models. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SpecCal consistently improves top-k reconstruction at both SMILES and scaffold levels across different base models. Further analyses demonstrate that calibrating candidate sets under spectral ambiguity provides a practical way to improve molecular reconstruction from IR spectra. The code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecCal-B18A.

</details>


### [170] [Certifying when decision-time information justifies adaptive experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27651)
*Jia Bi,Samuel Pinilla,Chenyang Zhu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出机会感知的实验室政策授权框架OPAL，在实验前决定是否启用自适应，通过预承诺合约保证非平凡适应、风险受控和正执行价值，并在限制性条件转移下实现目标校准恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应实验方法多在适应已允许后开始，缺乏在实验前评估是否应启用适应性的决策机制，可能导致无效或高风险的适应。

Method: OPAL框架采用预承诺合约，要求非平凡适应、目标风险受控且扣除成本后执行价值为正；推导出在无限制条件结果转移下无法统一支持非平凡授权的不可行边界，并给出目标校准恢复方法。

Result: 在11,265化合物细胞绘画分区上，冻结门控选择595个化合物，捕获384个阳性机会，最不利完成下执行价值严格为正，假激活上界5.18%低于7.5%限制；六种方法中仅OPAL同时实现非零激活与风险控制。

Conclusion: 授权作为安全自适应科学的独立层，通过区分政策错位与不可认证性，证明其在实验前置决策中的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Adaptive laboratories choose measurements during experiments, yet most methods begin after adaptation is permitted. We introduce Opportunity-aware Policy Authorization for Laboratories (\OPAL{}), a framework that decides whether adaptation should be enabled at all. \OPAL{} uses a precommitted contract to require non-trivial adaptation, controlled target risk and positive executed value after cost. We establish an impossibility boundary: source outcomes and unlabelled target covariates cannot uniformly support non-trivial authorization under unrestricted conditional outcome shift, and derive a target-calibrated recovery. Applied to an unseen 11,265-compound Cell Painting partition, the frozen gate selected 595 compounds, captured 384 positive opportunities and achieved strictly positive executed value under least-favourable completion; its 5.18\% false-activation upper bound remained below a 7.5\% limit. Among six methods, only \OPAL{} combined non-zero activation with this risk control. Locked pharmacogenomic and finite-campaign studies distinguish policy misalignment from non-certifiability, establishing authorization as a distinct layer for safe adaptive science.

</details>


### [171] [Annotating Topical Legal Insights from Case Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27792)
*Subinay Adhikary,Dwaipayan Roy,Debasis Ganguly,Shouvik Kumar Guha,Kripabandhu Ghosh*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDA系统，一个用于法律文档概念标注的网络平台，支持动态创建标签，已用于印度最高法院案件的概念标注与语义表示构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文档采用结构化表示（如概念集合）而非平面词袋，能显著提升处理能力，因此需要从法律案件记录中提取概念或主题。

Method: LeDA系统提供通用标注与裁决功能，通过网页界面操作；其新颖之处在于支持动态创建新标签，适用于无预定义本体、需标注者边审阅边发现概念的场景。

Result: 系统已被3名评估者用于标注和裁决印度最高法院案件中的法律概念名称，并构建文档的语义表示为概念集合。

Conclusion: LeDA可用于构建法律文档的概念级表示，支撑先例检索、判决预测等下游任务。

Abstract: In this paper, we mainly concentrate on finding concepts or topics from the legal case proceedings, since adopting a structured representation for legal documents, as opposed to a mere bag-of-words flat text representation, can significantly enhance processing capabilities. To achieve this objective, we put forward a set of diverse concepts for legal case proceedings. With this motivation, we propose LeDA, a system for Legal Data Annotation. The system offers the generic functionality of annotating and adjudicating entities or concepts within documents via a web-based interface. A novel feature of our system is that it allows to dynamic create new tags for annotation, which is a particularly useful provision for situations where there exists no pre-defined ontology for the entities (concepts) that need to be annotated - these being rather discovered by annotators as they continue examining more documents. The system that we demonstrate is currently in use to annotate a set of concepts from legal documents to construct semantic representations of documents as bags of concepts that can then be used for several downstream tasks, such as prior case retrieval, judgment prediction, and so on. Along with the system features in general, we also describe how LeDA was used by 3 assessors to annotate and adjudicate legal concept names from Indian Supreme Court case proceedings.

</details>


### [172] [Evaluation Protocols and Cross-Subject Generalization in EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27655)
*Hanting Suo,Yuwen Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: EEG情绪识别的准确率受评估流程影响，而非仅分类器。以DGCNN在SEED/SEED-IV为例，发现训练参与者与完全留出参与者的准确率差距巨大，且不同评估设置回答不同问题，应分别报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常混淆目标量、开发流程和报告规则，导致准确率比较不可靠，需系统解耦这些因素以揭示真实泛化能力。

Method: 采用固定DGCNN通路，进行协议匹配的受试者依赖检查、30条匹配轨迹的检查点选择分析、五折受试者不相交评估，以及支持性分析（表征/时间尺度影响、尾部风险集成）。

Result: 受试者依赖SEED结果与公开值差1.47个百分点；验证选择检查点在训练参与者上达0.9990（SEED）/0.9920（SEED-IV），但完全留出参与者仅0.5348（SEED）/0.3954（SEED-IV），差距不支持欠拟合解释。

Conclusion: 训练到留出受试者的巨大差距提示评估范式决定结论，受试者依赖、受试者不相交和跨会话结果应视为不同问题分别报告，避免单一指标误导。

Abstract: Reported accuracy in electroencephalography (EEG) emotion recognition depends on the complete evaluation procedure, not only the classifier. We separate the target quantity, development procedure, and reporting rule, then use one archived dynamical graph convolutional neural network (DGCNN) pathway on SEED and SEED-IV as an illustrative case. In a protocol-matched subject-dependent check, the SEED result was within 1.47 percentage points of the public reference value; the 3.40-point SEED-IV difference remained unresolved. Across 30 matched SEED subject-session trajectories, checkpoint selection based on repeated test-set evaluation increased mean window accuracy from 0.7855 at epoch 80 to 0.8892. Under five-fold subject-disjoint evaluation, validation-selected checkpoints achieved training-participant trial accuracies of 0.9990 on SEED and 0.9920 on SEED-IV. Accuracy for entirely held-out participants was 0.5348 (95% conditional subject-level bias-corrected and accelerated [BCa] interval [0.4667, 0.5985]) on SEED. The SEED-IV estimate was 0.3954 ([0.3343, 0.4648]) and is reported only as secondary sensitivity evidence because its protocol-matched compatibility check remained unresolved. The observed train-to-held-out-subject gaps are inconsistent with simple optimization underfitting, but they do not isolate subject identity from implementation, preprocessing, representation, or distributional factors. Supporting analyses further showed that participant rankings depended on representation and time scale, while a development-selected tail-risk ensemble did not establish a positive gain in a separate final evaluation. Subject-dependent, subject-disjoint, and cross-session results should therefore be reported as answers to different questions.

</details>


### [173] [Tight Sample Complexity for Low-Rank Adaptation: Matching Bounds and Rank Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27680)
*Arunan J*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过局部 Rademacher 论证和 Fano 型下界，完整刻画了 LoRA 微调的统计复杂度，证明了秩 r 的 excess risk 上界和下界均为 O~(rd/n)，并据此提出秩选择二分法：约束经验风险最小化时最优秩等于内在秩 r*，过参数化严格有害；而核范数截断类自适应估计对过参数化鲁棒，速率饱和于 Θ~(r* d / n)。实验结果验证了理论预测的 U 形验证损失曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 已成为大模型微调的标准方法，但其统计性质理解不完整：现有上界缺乏匹配下界，且 LoRA 秩 r 的选择缺乏形式化指导。本文旨在填补这两项空白，并解释实践中观察到的过参数化惩罚现象。

Method: 采用局部 Rademacher 复杂度论证推导上界 O~(rd/n)；通过 Fano 型 packing 构造秩 r 子空间的下界 Ω(rd/n)；基于上下界匹配提出秩选择二分法，分别分析约束经验风险最小化与核范数截断类自适应估计；最后在合成迹回归和真实 LoRA 微调任务（DistilBERT、RoBERTa 于 SST-2、MRPC）上验证理论预测。

Result: 上界 O~(rd/n) 与下界 Ω(rd/n) 匹配；约束 ERM 的秩选择二分法成立，过参数化严格有害；核范数截断类自适应估计过参数化无害且速率饱和于 Θ~(r* d / n)；真实实验显示所有配置均出现预测的 U 形验证损失，两个配置在较大秩时损失显著增大。

Conclusion: LoRA 的统计复杂度在局部二次良设定场景下为 Θ~(rd/n)；过参数化惩罚源于未正则化的经验风险最小化而非 LoRA 类本身；秩选择策略需区分估计器类型，约束 ERM 应选择内在秩，而自适应估计可安全过参数化。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard mechanism for fine-tuning large pretrained models, yet its statistical properties remain only partially understood. Existing generalization results provide upper bounds of the form O~(sqrt(rd/n)) or O~(rd/n), but a matching lower bound is missing, and the question of how to choose the LoRA rank r has no formal answer. Both gaps are closed here. A local Rademacher argument establishes an upper bound of O~(rd/n) on the excess risk of the empirical risk minimizer over rank-r LoRA, whenever the target adaptation has rank at most r. A matching minimax lower bound of Omega(rd/n) is then proved via a Fano-type packing of the rank-r subspace of R^{d x d}; the bound applies to any estimator whose output lies in the rank-r LoRA class. Combining the two yields a rank-selection dichotomy. For the constrained empirical risk minimizer, the optimal rank equals the intrinsic rank r*, and over-ranking strictly hurts. For adaptive estimators of the nuclear-norm-then-truncate type, over-ranking is harmless and the rate saturates at Theta~(r* d / n) regardless of r. Taken together, the three results characterize the statistical complexity of LoRA fine-tuning within the well-specified locally quadratic regime, and identify the empirically observed over-parameterization penalty as a property of unregularized empirical risk minimization rather than of the LoRA class itself. Predictions of the theory are verified on a synthetic trace-regression benchmark and on real LoRA fine-tuning across three (model, task) configurations covering DistilBERT and RoBERTa on SST-2 and MRPC. All configurations exhibit the predicted U-shape in validation loss, with two showing statistically significant loss inflation at large ranks (paired permutation p = 0.016).

</details>


### [174] [Simplifying Neural Networks During Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27854)
*Lorenzo Sciandra,Samuele Fonio,Roberto Esposito*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经坍缩（NC）的深度网络简化训练框架，通过逆Fisher准则监控表示动态，自动确定特征提取与分类的分割点及可简化训练阶段，替换尾部层为轻量分类头，在保持精度的同时显著减少参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络训练动态难以理解，但神经坍缩和隧道效应显示网络结构存在冗余，仅部分层对特征提取关键，因此可利用这些结构特性在训练中简化网络，降低参数开销。

Method: 结合NC与隧道效应，使用逆Fisher准则作为可变性坍缩的稳定代理指标，监测表示动态以确定特征提取与分类的分割点及合适简化时机，随后将尾部层替换为轻量分类头并继续训练简化模型。

Result: 在MLP、VGG和ResNet架构的图像分类基准上，该方法实现大幅参数缩减，同时精度与完整模型相当。

Conclusion: NC启发的训练时网络简化策略有效，既能保持性能又能减少模型规模，提供了利用训练动态进行高效网络设计的可行途径。

Abstract: Understanding and exploiting the training dynamics of overparameterized deep neural networks remains a central challenge in modern machine learning. Recent evidence on Neural Collapse (NC) shows that class representations and classifiers exhibit highly structured geometry, while the Tunnel Effect suggests that only a subset of layers is essential for feature extraction. We combine these two perspectives and propose an NC-inspired training framework for simplifying deep networks during training. Our method monitors representation dynamics through the Inverse Fisher Criterion, a stable and efficient proxy for the variability collapse behavior, to identify both the split point between feature extraction and classification and the training stage at which simplification becomes viable. We then replace the trailing layers with a lightweight classification head and continue training the reduced model. Experiments on image-classification benchmarks across MLP, VGG, and ResNet architectures show that the proposed method achieves substantial parameter reductions while maintaining accuracy comparable to that of the full model. Code to reproduce the experiments can be found at: https://github.com/LorenzoSciandra/NNS.

</details>


### [175] [Back to All-Entity Ranking: Sampler-Dependent Evaluation in Continuous-Time Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27861)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 连续时间动态图中的下一目的地预测通常使用采样负样本，但非均匀负采样分布会改变贝叶斯最优排序，且即使均匀采样有限候选集也会干扰模型排名和模块效应测量；全实体排序可消除负采样自由度，推荐作为主要评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTDG基准使用采样负目的地评估，导致评分条件依赖于负样本分布和候选数量，可能产生有偏的模型比较和消融结论。

Method: 通过因子化评估（重复/新正样本 × 已见/未见负样本）、基于对历史成员关系的最小评分器、受控表征干预，在LastFM、MOOC、Reddit和Wikipedia上对六种模型进行系统分析，并比较Uniform-20与全目录排序及不同候选集大小和训练目标下的模块效应。

Result: 在四个数据集中有三个，至少一对模型的相对排序在Uniform-20和全目录间发生变化；同一模块的效应大小和方向随候选集大小和训练目标改变。

Conclusion: 基于采样负样本的基准结论依赖于候选配置；对于具有可枚举固定目的地目录的CTDG基准，应使用全实体排序作为架构比较的主要证据，以消除负采样选择自由度和采样变异。

Abstract: Next-destination prediction in continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDGs) commonly ranks an observed interaction against sampled negative destinations. The resulting score is conditional on both the negative distribution and the number of candidates chosen by the researcher. We show that a non-uniform negative distribution changes the Bayes-optimal ranking, while even a finite candidate set drawn uniformly can destabilize model rankings and measured module effects.
  Time-varying source-destination history membership and model operations that use this information directly transmit the sampler's influence to the evaluation score. We examine this mechanism using a factorial evaluation of repeated and new positives against seen and unseen negatives, a minimal scorer based solely on pair-history membership, and controlled representation interventions. Across six models on LastFM, MOOC, Reddit, and Wikipedia, at least one model pair changes relative order between the expected Uniform-20 metric and the full catalog on three of the four datasets. The measured effect of the same module also changes in magnitude and direction with the candidate-set size and training objective.
  These results establish that model-superiority and ablation conclusions from sampled-negative benchmarks are conditional on the stated candidate configuration. All-entity ranking evaluates every destination in a fixed catalog, eliminating negative-selection freedom and sampling variation while retaining the original CTDG scorer. We therefore recommend all-entity ranking as the primary evidence for architecture comparisons on CTDG benchmarks with an enumerable, fixed destination catalog.

</details>


### [176] [Towards joint scaling laws with optimal batch size schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27731)
*Jiaxiang Li,Zhiqi Bu,Shiyun Xu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过凸优化视角，推导出损失函数关于学习率和批大小调度的联合表征，并给出任意学习率下的最优批大小闭式调度，所提联合缩放定律在LLM训练中优于静态批大小基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习训练通常固定批大小，忽略了学习率与批大小对训练动态的联合影响，导致训练效率与最终性能未达最优。

Method: 从凸优化角度建立损失与两种调度之间的联合理论表征，适用于通用优化器和模型架构；基于该表征求解给定学习率下的最优批大小闭式调度，并据此构建联合缩放定律。

Result: 联合缩放定律在多组实验上一致优于静态批大小基线，显著提升了大语言模型训练的效率与性能。

Conclusion: 动态批大小调度在深度学习训练中具有关键作用，联合调度策略为大型模型训练提供了更优的理论指导与实践方案。

Abstract: Modern deep learning typically keeps the batch size static throughout training, thus overlooking the joint effect of learning rate and batch size on the training dynamics. In this paper, we study the deep learning dynamics through the lens of convex optimization and derive a joint characterization of loss in terms of both schedules, applicable to general optimizers and model architectures. This characterization yields a closed-form optimal batch size schedule for any prescribed learning rate schedule, and further leads to joint scaling laws that consistently outperform static batch size baselines, highlighting the significance of dynamic batch size schedule in large language model training.

</details>


### [177] [DAS-PMVC: A Framework for Partial Multi-View Clustering via Dual Alignment and Structure Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27761)
*Shubin Ma,Liang Zhao,Chuanye He,Zhenjiao Liu,Liang Zou,Lin Yuanbo Wu,Yu Shao*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出DAS-PMVC框架，通过双对齐与结构增强解决部分多视图聚类中的视图不对齐问题，实验验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图数据常因采集设备限制导致视图间不对齐，即部分视图对齐问题（PVAP），现有方法难以有效缓解视图不对称与无关样本的影响。

Method: 包含三部分：锚点图结构对齐（利用锚点关系生成一致潜在空间的联合嵌入表示）、结构增强特征学习（预训练学习视图结构信息并结合多视图图卷积网络提取深层特征）、双对齐策略（预训练阶段锚点图初始对齐，训练阶段引入对比学习损失与匈牙利算法优化潜在特征对齐）。

Result: 在多个数据集上，DAS-PMVC的聚类性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DAS-PMVC有效解决部分多视图聚类中的对齐问题，展现出显著的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: In recent years, multi-view clustering has attracted widespread research interest. However, due to limitations in data collection devices, data across different views often suffer from misalignment, leading to the partial view alignment problem (PVAP). To mitigate the impact of view asymmetry and irrelevant samples, this paper proposes a framework for partial multi-view clustering via dual alignment and structure enhancement (DAS-PMVC), which leverages view structure consistency and semantic relevance. Specifically, DAS-PMVC includes three parts: \textbf{anchor graph structure alignment}, where sample joint embedding representations with consistent latent space are derived from anchor point relationships for initial view alignment; \textbf{structure-enhanced feature learning}, where the model learns view structure information through pretraining and combines multi-view graph convolutional networks to further extract deep latent features from the aligned graph structure to improve the discriminative power of representations; and \textbf{a dual alignment strategy}, where initial alignment is performed through the anchor graph in the pretraining phase, and contrastive learning loss and the Hungarian algorithm are introduced in the training phase to further optimize the alignment of latent features. Experimental results on various datasets demonstrate that the DAS-PMVC framework outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in clustering performance, showcasing its effectiveness and superiority.

</details>


### [178] [Train Small, Deploy Large: Zero-Shot GNN Transfer Through Geometric Renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27767)
*Robert Jankowski,Pedro Almagro-Blanco,Marián Boguñá,Melanie Weber,M. Ángeles Serrano*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出零样本迁移协议，在几何重整化缩小的图副本上训练GNN，直接迁移权重至原图，在保持大部分预测性能的同时显著降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模图上的GNN训练对基础设施敏感，需要可扩展训练技术，但现有方法未充分利用图的结构自相似性。作者希望验证能否在缩小图上训练并直接部署于原图，以降低训练开销。

Method: 使用几何重整化（GR）生成图的粗粒化缩尺副本，在该副本上训练GNN，随后将训练好的权重零样本迁移到原始全分辨率图，无需微调。在合成和真实网络上评估性能、表征对齐性和训练成本。

Result: 在多种网络上，缩尺副本训练能保留大部分原始预测性能，显著减少训练成本；学习到的表征与预测轨迹在跨尺度上保持一致。

Conclusion: 结构相似性比网络规模更关键地决定GNN的可迁移性，为构建尺度等变的图架构提供了新方向。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can operate on large graphs but become infrastructure-sensitive at the scale of millions of nodes and typically require scalable training techniques for even larger graphs. This raises a central question: when can a model trained on a smaller, scaled-down replica of a graph be deployed on the full-resolution graph without retraining? We introduce a zero-shot transfer protocol in which a GNN is trained on a graph coarse-grained by geometric renormalization (GR), and the resulting weights are transferred directly to the original network. Across synthetic and real-world networks, training on GR scaled-down replicas preserves much of the original-scale predictive performance while significantly reducing training cost. We further find that learned representations and predictive trajectories remain aligned across scales. These findings suggest that structural similarity may be more important than network size in determining GNN transferability, opening a path toward scale-equivariant graph architectures.

</details>


### [179] [Meta-Task: Turning Terminal Task Synthesis into a Terminal Task for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27929)
*Zhihong Pan,Jiyuan He,Kai Zhang,Yupeng Han,Ze Liu,Yuze Zhao,Yongcong Ye,Zhaohua Yang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Task框架，将终端任务合成本身建模为Terminal-Bench格式任务，在真实容器内迭代生成、执行和验证，实现高可靠性、多样性和可扩展性的训练数据合成。仅用3,221条轨迹微调Qwen3模型，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上分别达到22.5%和31.8%的Avg Pass@1，超越同类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端任务合成方法存在两个关键瓶颈：任务生成与实际执行脱节导致可靠性差，以及依赖已有代码库导致多样性和可扩展性受限。

Method: 核心创新是将任务合成重新定义为Terminal-Bench格式任务本身，让智能体在真实容器环境中迭代生成、执行和验证任务，确保生成组件在循环内经过一致性和可执行性检查。在此之上沿多维度解耦任务需求，采用多阶段机制动态设计新颖任务规格，并可选引入外部材料增强多样性和真实性，最后用LLM-as-Judge过滤保证训练数据质量。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，仅使用3,221条Meta-Task合成轨迹微调，Qwen3-14B和Qwen3-32B分别获得22.5%和31.8%的Avg Pass@1，以显著更少的训练数据超越同期方法。

Conclusion: 验证了将任务合成视为可执行终端任务本身的有效性，表明通过生成-执行-验证闭环和分阶段解耦设计，能以更少数据实现更强的终端代理性能，为终端任务数据规模化合成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Training terminal agents at scale requires diverse, verifiable terminal tasks and high-quality interaction trajectories, yet acquiring such data remains a significant challenge. Existing synthesis methods face two key limitations: (1) weak reliability caused by the disconnect between task generation and real execution, and (2) limited diversity and scalability due to dependence on existing repositories. We propose Meta-Task, a framework that redefines terminal task synthesis as a Terminal-Bench-format task itself: an agent operates within a real container environment to iteratively generate, execute, and verify tasks, so that synthesized components are checked for internal consistency and executability within the generation loop itself. Building upon this, we decouple the target task requirements along multiple dimensions, introduce a multi-phase mechanism that dynamically designs novel task specifications before producing the actual tasks, and incorporate optional external material support to enhance diversity and realism. We additionally apply LLM-as-Judge filtering to ensure the quality of the final training data. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.0 show that fine-tuning on only 3,221 Meta-Task synthesized trajectories achieves 22.5% and 31.8% Avg Pass@1 for Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B respectively, outperforming concurrent approaches with significantly less training data.

</details>


### [180] [Contrastive Concept Importance: Explaining Pairwise Class Decisions Through Automatically Extracted Concept Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27904)
*Roel Visser,Isaac Roberts,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出对比概念重要性（CCI）方法，用于解释模型为何选择目标类别而非对照类别，通过分解logit差值识别支持或反对某类别的概念，并区分全局重要性与类别对特异的对比效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念归因方法仅回答非对比性的“为什么是P？”，但在误分类、类别混淆或低置信度场景中，更需要解释“为什么是P而不是Q？”的对比性问题。

Method: CCI将目标类别与对照（foil）类别之间的logit差值归因于自动提取的视觉概念基中的概念，产生有符号分数（正负表示支持哪一方），并可进一步分解为目标logit和foil logit的贡献，以区分共享、单侧或直接对比效应。

Result: 在ImageNet类别对上使用CRAFT式概念基、插入/删除曲线、logit分解和语义层级评估，显示CCI能揭示普通概念重要性无法捕捉的类别对特异行为，且高对比概念与语义超类结构相符，反映细粒度区分而非宽泛类别证据。

Conclusion: 对比概念重要性提供更细粒度的模型解释，尤其适用于类别混淆与低裕度预测，可增强对模型决策机制的理解。

Abstract: Concept-based explanations are a prevalent way to explain the decisions of complex black-box methods through semantically meaningful, human-interpretable concepts. To attribute the contribution of such concepts to a model's decisions, feature attribution methods are used to quantify how strongly each concept contributes to a model output. These attributions are typically computed for a single output class and therefore answer a non-contrastive "why P?" question. In many situations, however, such as cases of misclassification, class confusion, and low-margin predictions, the more natural question to ask is "why P rather than Q?".
  We introduce contrastive concept importance (CCI), which attributes the logit margin between a target class and a contrast, or foil, class to concepts in an automatically extracted visual concept basis. The resulting scores are signed, indicating whether a concept supports the target over the foil or the foil over the target, and can be decomposed into target-logit and foil-logit effects. This makes it possible to distinguish globally important concepts from concepts that specifically influence a class-pair distinction, including whether their effect is shared, one-sided, or directly contrastive.
  We evaluate the method on ImageNet class pairs using CRAFT-style concept bases, insertion and deletion curves, logit-wise decomposition analysis, and semantic class hierarchy. The results show that contrastive concept importance reveals class-pair-specific model behavior that is not captured by ordinary concept importance alone, and that highly contrastive concepts can be evaluated against semantic superclass structure to assess whether they affect fine-grained distinctions rather than broad category evidence.

</details>


### [181] [Class-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Counterfactual Explanation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27905)
*Muhammad Adil Saleem,Syed Ali Raza,Mary-Anne Williams*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究首次将实例预测类别纳入强化学习状态表示以生成反事实解释，相比类盲方法在收敛速度、奖励优化、有效性及生成质量上均显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的反事实解释方法仅使用监督数据中的特征构造状态，忽视实例预测类别信息，旨在探索类别感知是否能提升探索效率与策略最优性。

Method: 提出类别感知强化学习方法，在状态表示中融合预测类别与原始特征，并与排除类别信息的类盲强化学习进行对比，在七个不同规模数据集上用SHAP和LIME分析特征重要性。

Result: 类别感知RL在训练收敛速度、奖励优化和回合长度缩减上表现更优，生成有效反事实数量显著增加；类别特征在动作选择中重要性排名靠前。

Conclusion: 类别感知状态表示能增强RL生成反事实的清晰度、学习效率与有效性，适用于多样化数据集，为CFE生成提供新方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CFEs) enhance the interpretability of black-box models by generating alternative instances with adjusted feature values that achieve a contrastive outcome. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising approach for CFE generation, enabling efficient exploration of counterfactual instances while ensuring control over key metrics like validity, sparsity, and proximity. Previous studies have formulated RL states exclusively using features derived from the predictors in the supervised dataset. This study explores the impact of including an instance's predicted class, alongside features derived from the predictors, in the RL state representation for generating CFEs. The hypothesis is that class-awareness enhances exploration efficiency and improves policy optimality. We compare the proposed class-aware RL method with the class-blind RL method, which is similar but excludes the instance's class information from the state representation. The comparison was conducted using seven datasets from diverse domains, varying in size. The results show that during training, class-aware RL offers benefits in terms of convergence speed, reward optimization, and episode length reduction. Moreover, it generates significantly more valid CFEs compared to class-blind RL. Finally, the instance's class-based feature consistently ranks among the most influential predictors in RL's action-selection, as shown by the SHAP and LIME values, underscoring the significance of class-awareness in RL for CFE generation. The impact is heightened clarity, faster learning, improved validity, and more effective counterfactual generation across diverse datasets.

</details>


### [182] [Shapes from Examples: Foundations of Shape Learning in Recursive SHACL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27934)
*Bente Gortworst,Cem Okulmus,Magdalena Ortiz,Anni-Yasmin Turhan*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究了SHACL形状学习中基于拟合的方法：给定正负示例节点集合，计算一个用描述逻辑ELI核心片段（递归形状目录）表达的SHACL形状，使其验证所有正例且拒绝所有负例。作者针对良基、稳定、支持三种目录语义，解决了拟合存在性与最特定拟合计算问题，建立了两个问题的紧指数时间上界，并为重要特例获得多项式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHACL形状用于数据图验证，自动学习形状对知识图谱应用至关重要。现有工作缺乏对用ELI片段（对应描述逻辑ELI）表达的递归形状目录在不同语义下的系统研究，尤其是拟合存在性和最特定拟合计算的复杂度边界尚不明确。

Method: 聚焦SHACL核心片段（对应DL ELI），形式化定义形状表达式及递归目录的三种语义（良基、稳定、支持）。针对拟合存在性（判断是否存在形状区分正负例）和最特定拟合计算（计算最具体的满足条件的形状）分别给出算法，并分析其时间复杂度。

Result: 拟合存在性和最特定拟合计算在一般情形下均具有紧的指数时间上界；在特定限制（如目录无递归或表达式结构受限）下，问题可在多项式时间内解决。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于拟合的SHACL形状学习奠定了复杂度理论基础，明确了ELI片段下问题的难易边界，为实际算法设计与优化提供了指导方向。

Abstract: SHACL shapes enable data graph validation, making automatic shape learning essential for knowledge graph applications. We investigate the well-known fitting approach to this task: given sets P and N of positive and negative example nodes from an input graph, compute a shape expression C, possibly using shape names defined in a recursive shape catalogue, that validates at every node in P and none in N. We focus on the case where C is written in a core fragment of SHACL corresponding to the Description Logic ELI. For the catalogue, we consider the well-founded, stable, and supported semantics. We address fitting existence and most specific fitting computation, establish tight exponential-time upper bounds for both problems, and obtain polynomial bounds for relevant special cases.

</details>


### [183] [Share the Judge, Learn the Deferral: Where Specialization Helps LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27984)
*Weining Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 研究发现评估器应共享判断学习，而将领域适配放在可审计的放行边界中，而非内置到LoRA权重中；共享初始化可大幅提升性能，级联路由方法在RewardBench 2上以较低计算量提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent系统在生成候选输出与审查之间差距扩大，需解决领域专业化应通过评估器权重还是可信规则来实现的架构问题。

Method: 基于99,952个带rubric的公共示例进行对照实验，比较rubric提示、按标准族划分的8个LoRA专家、共享初始化的LoRA以及学习型正确性头路由的级联模型，并通过风险审计验证放行规则。

Result: rubric正确提示提升2.11分，无关rubric损失2.66分；8个LoRA专家损失10.05分，覆盖率从24.44%降至5.43%，但共享初始化恢复至76.85%（比随机初始化高19.94分）。级联在RewardBench 2上以0.415参数计算量达89.40%准确率，优于8B模型的84.75%，且通过风险审计。

Conclusion: 建议在数据不足时共享判断学习，领域适配应置于可审计的放行边界而非权重的局部划分，形成有条件的架构设计规则。

Abstract: Agentic systems have widened the gap between producing candidate outputs and reviewing them. This paper asks a practical architectural question: should domain specialization be built into an evaluator's weights, or into the rule that decides when its judgment can be trusted? We study 99,952 public, rubric-conditioned examples. Supplying the correct rubric improves locked-test accuracy by 2.11 points over a response-only control; replacing it with an unrelated rubric costs 2.66 points. Dividing the same training corpus among eight criterion-family LoRA judges, however, loses 10.05 points and cuts audited coverage at a 5% risk target from 24.44% to 5.43%. Matching the bank's stored capacity with one rank-64 adapter does not reproduce this loss. Nor is the result explained by learning rate or optimizer steps. Initializing the family adapters from a shared, trained judge recovers test accuracy to 76.85%, 19.94 points above scratch training at the same learning rate (95% interval 18.88-21.02). The result changes when specialization governs deferral rather than judgment. On RewardBench 2, learned correctness heads route examples through a 0.6B-4B-8B cascade without changing any reward score. Across 20 locked repartitions, the cascade attains 89.40% accuracy, compared with 84.75% for 8B alone, at 0.415 normalized parameter compute. Every run passes an exact one-sided 95% risk audit; margin-based rules remain near 84.8% accuracy while using at least 0.94 compute. These results suggest a qualified design rule: share the learning of judgment until there is enough data to justify a split, and place domain-specific adaptation in an audited release boundary.

</details>


### [184] [Harnessing the Potential of Optimizing Data Mixtures via Bayesian Domain Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27928)
*Xiang Yuan,Kaiqing Lei,Zhenyu Jin,Jun Shu,Deyu Meng,Zongben Xu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯域加权方法，利用Gamma先验推断Dirichlet分布权重，实现稳定高效的多领域预训练数据配比优化，相比搜索式函数拟合方法大幅降低数据消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域加权方法依赖强结构假设（如秩不变性或缩放定律），易产生估计偏差；直接优化方法虽无需假设但存在优化轨迹不稳定和计算开销大等问题，难以扩展至大规模应用。

Method: 采用贝叶斯框架，将域权重建模为Dirichlet分布，并引入从观测数据中学习的Gamma先验信息来推断权重，形成先验引导的优化过程。

Result: 实验表明该方法能实现稳定高效的域权重学习，找到最优数据混合配比，且比基于搜索的函数拟合法消耗显著更少的数据。

Conclusion: 该方法复兴了基于优化的域加权技术，使其适用于大规模预训练场景，为多领域数据配比优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) is fundamentally influenced by the distributional composition of multi-domain pre-training data. While manual heuristics were prevalent in early models, they increasingly fail to capture the intricate synergies between domains as data complexity grows. To overcome the issue, a dominant approach seeks to fit a proxy function mapping between domain weights and their corresponding validation losses, and then find the optimal domain weights to minimize validation losses. These methods rely on strong structural assumptions, such as rank invariance or scaling laws, which are often violated, resulting in non-negligible estimation bias. A promising approach is to directly optimize the weighting scheme from data. However, it suffers from unstable optimization trajectory and prohibitive computational overhead, limiting its potential to search better domain weights configurations. This paper presents a Bayesian domain weighting method to infer the weights from a Dirichlet distribution via introducing Gamma prior information learned from observations. Experimental results demonstrate that proposed method could achieve stable and efficient domain weights learning, and identifies optimal mixtures while consuming substantially less data than search-based function-fitting methods, revitalizing optimization-based domain weighting for large-scale applications.

</details>


### [185] [MMLDSum-LLM: Multimodal Long-Document Summarization with Visual-Alignment and Keyword-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28006)
