We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [221] [Driving up Inference Energy on SNNs: Per-Sample and Universal Sponge Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27990)
*Spyridon Raptis,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文发现脉冲神经网络（SNN）的稀疏二进制事件通信优势同时引入安全风险：海绵攻击可增加推理时脉冲活动与突触工作量，导致能量消耗膨胀且难以通过正确性监控检测。作者提出两种攻击模型：逐样本梯度优化海绵攻击（SynOps增加1.5-2.6倍）与首个原生事件输入通用海绵攻击（XOR固定扰动，SynOps增加1.09-1.24倍），并映射至Loihi-1能耗显示每次推理额外开销达14μJ至13.24mJ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在神经形态硬件上高效节能，广泛用于始终开启的边缘系统；但效率优势可能被恶意利用。已有输入空间效率攻击仅针对逐样本速率编码场景，缺乏对原生事件输入（如二进制脉冲序列）的系统研究，且通用攻击缺失。需评估此类攻击在真实部署中的可行性与能量影响。

Method: 提出两类攻击：(1)逐样本海绵攻击：对每个输入通过梯度优化生成定制对抗脉冲序列，以最大化脉冲活动或SynOps；(2)通用海绵攻击：离线计算固定二进制扰动，推理时对输入施加XOR操作，无需逐样本优化。在两模型（NMNIST、SHD、IBM DVS Gesture）上评估，并基于Loihi-1功耗模型估算能量开销。

Result: 逐样本攻击在三个数据集上SynOps提升1.5-2.6倍，且至少98%样本保持原类别；通用攻击SynOps提升1.09-1.24倍。映射到Loihi-1后，每次推理能量额外消耗14μJ至13.24mJ，证明攻击可造成显著电池损耗。

Conclusion: 原生事件SNN在实际环境中面临输入空间效率攻击的实质威胁；通用扰动虽效果较弱但更现实，因可持续累积导致边缘系统电池快速耗尽，呼吁设计能抵抗此类能量侧信道攻击的SNN防御机制。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spike events rather than dense activations, enabling energy-efficient inference on neuromorphic hardware and motivating their use in always-on, battery-powered edge systems. We show that this same efficiency advantage creates a distinct security risk: sponge attacks can increase inference-time spike activity and synaptic workload, inflating energy consumption while remaining difficult to detect through correctness-based monitoring alone. Prior input-space efficiency attacks on SNNs have focused on per-sample optimization, primarily in rate-coded settings. We extend this threat to native event-based binary inputs and study two attack models. First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [222] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 首次在脉冲神经网络上实现干净标签时间后门攻击，通过固定时间戳变换保留事件计数却改变序列，在多种数据集和网络架构下达到100%攻击成功率，并分析了防御边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN后门攻击多基于脏标签投毒，而干净标签攻击更隐蔽。该研究旨在探索仅对目标类训练流施加时间变换的干净标签投毒，检验时间维度信息在SNN中的脆弱性，并研究现有防御的失效场景。

Method: 提出一种固定时间戳变换，仅作用于目标类训练数据而不改变标签；该变换逐像素逐极性事件计数完全不变，但改变SNN处理的时序。攻击在卷积和Transformer架构、三个神经形态数据集上验证；同时设计基于每步事件质量的模型无关检测器，对比时间轴折叠防御与特征空间防御。

Result: 最强配置下攻击成功率（ASR）达到1.00；时间折叠类防御因构造盲目失效，特征空间防御仅在选定设置下检测到投毒；提出的检测器可有效检测所评估的时间变换，同时揭示攻击隐蔽性的边界。

Conclusion: 该研究首次评估SNN上的干净标签后门攻击，证明仅修改时序即可绕过基于统计聚合的防御，并为后续防御设计提供新方向，强调在脉冲模型中必须保留时间结构分析。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [223] [Checking Information Flow in Cloud-based IoT Access Control Policies (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28088)
*Lorenzo Ceragioli,Letterio Galletta,Edoardo Lunati*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出IOT:POKER工具，通过形式化建模AWS IoT Core并构建信息流图，利用SMT求解器实现有限表示，以验证设备间非预期信息流并识别访问控制策略中的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云IoT平台的访问控制配置对安全至关重要，但孤立验证权限不足以应对设备间不同信任级别或子系统隔离的场景，存在非预期信息流导致的安全隐患。

Method: 形式化建模AWS IoT Core组件，定义信息流图捕获策略允许的设备通信；通过SMT求解器构建图的有限表示，从而支持信息流验证，并实现为工具IOT:POKER。

Result: 在现实场景和多个真实世界策略上评估了IOT:POKER，成功识别出潜在的信息流安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效分析IoT访问控制策略中的非预期信息流，提升安全验证能力，为云IoT环境提供实用漏洞检测手段。

Abstract: Many cloud providers for IoT technologies offer access control mechanisms whose proper configuration is critical for security. However, verifying permissions in isolation is insufficient in a setting where devices have different levels of trust or are compartmentalised in various subsystems. This work analyses IoT access control policies to identify potential security vulnerabilities from unwanted information flow between devices. To this end, we formally model AWS IoT Core's components and define an information flow graph to capture the communication among devices permitted by the access control policies. We build a finite representation of the graph by leveraging an SMT solver, thus enabling the verification of information flow between devices. We implement our approach in a tool called IOT:POKER, and assess it on a realistic scenario and several real-world policies.

</details>


### [224] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Agent Harness Distillation (AHD)框架，研究从黑盒交互中提取自主多智能体系统推理时harness的知识产权泄露风险，并验证了一种基于欺骗的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注静态多智能体系统的IP泄露，而AMAS中harness行为在推理时动态涌现，其作为高价值IP的泄露风险尚未被探索。

Method: AHD两阶段提取：预蒸馏阶段从目标智能体响应中推断harness行为并构建初始harness；后蒸馏阶段迭代优化以对齐目标行为模式。

Result: 在多个真实AMAS和骨干LLM上的实验表明AHD有效，证实了显著的IP泄露风险，同时提出的欺骗防御能降低提取效果且保持智能体效用。

Conclusion: 揭示了AMAS中此前未被充分认识的安全威胁，为推理时harness的知识产权保护提供了新视角和防御思路。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks. AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [225] [Compression-Based Behavioral Similarity for Open-World Sybil Discovery on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27370)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于压缩相似度（NCD）的行为图框架，利用交易语法从EVM交易轨迹中提取行为特征，无需资金转移或监督训练即可发现Sybil候选者，并通过时间漂移和对抗扰动测试验证其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sybil检测依赖资金图或监督学习，难以应对攻击策略频繁变化和逃避手段；需要一种无需直接资金关联、无需监督训练且对时间漂移和对抗扰动鲁棒的开放性发现方法。

Method: 从EVM交易轨迹合成符号化交易语法，分别捕获交易节奏、执行结构和功能意图；通过“盲点协议”过滤高信号合约；使用Gzip基NCD构建行为图用于Sybil发现；对照监督学习基线、时间分割和合成伪装压力测试进行验证。

Result: 框架在无监督条件下能够有效区分Sybil机器人、有机用户和套利机器人钱包；对时间漂移和合成伪装攻击具有鲁棒性；NCD原语可在无资金关联下扩展可疑种子钱包。

Conclusion: 该方法作为无训练、局部发现的开放世界审计原语，优于闭集分类范式，适用于区块链审计中的Sybil候选发现，而非形式化的开放集识别系统。

Abstract: Sybil attackers are Blockchain actors that adopt the characteristics of regular users to exploit airdrops or influence governance. Current methods of Sybil actor detection include constructing graphs, which requires token transfers between examined wallets. Machine learning algorithms have been employed as well, but they treat the task as a closed-set classification problem, making them vulnerable to frequent changes in attack strategies or evasion tactics. We address the following questions: can compression-based similarity differentiate Sybil bots, organic users, and arbitrage bot wallets without direct financial links? What is the effect of high-signal contracts on the discovery of Sybils, and how robust are behavioral graphs under temporal drift and adversarial perturbations? Our approach synthesizes a symbolic Transaction Grammar from EVM (Ethereum Virtual Machine) traces, capturing separately transaction rhythm, execution structure, and functional intent. The high-signal contracts are filtered with our own protocol, called the Blind-Spot Protocol. Gzip-based NCD is used to construct a behavioral graph for Sybil discovery. We validate this framework against supervised machine learning baselines, a temporal split, and synthetic camouflage stress tests. Ultimately, we contribute a leakage-aware behavioral framework for Sybil candidate discovery. Its core NCD primitive requires no supervised training and can expand suspicious seed wallets without explicit funding links. We position the method as a training-free local discovery primitive for open-world blockchain audits, rather than as a formal open-set recognition system.

</details>


### [226] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出VAmoS Bench基准，通过模拟信用卡客服电话场景，端到端评估语音代理的‘包含率’（自主解决率），可捕获虚假操作或信息泄露等违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音代理基准多关注组件质量（如字错误率、延迟）和会话属性，缺乏对代理是否独立正确处理整通电话的评估，而联络中心重视‘包含率’，即系统无需人工介入的呼叫比例，且某些场景的正确结果应为拒绝或转接，现有基准无法覆盖。

Method: 建立名为VAmoS Bench的模拟平台，设定客服代理Riley处理信用卡冻结等任务，含100个带私有目标和PostgreSQL后端的场景，约三分之一包含对抗性压力；代理可通过五个工具执行真实SQL，每场景定义二元断言，由评分器依据完整对话与操作痕迹（含工具调用和返回数据）判定，支持版本演进的排行榜。

Result: 该基准能识别声称修改卡片但未更新数据库的代理，也能识别数据库操作正确但泄露受保护信息的代理；首批版本聚焦金融服务业，并设计为可扩展的评估协议。

Conclusion: VAmoS Bench填补了语音代理端到端任务正确性评估的空白，通过真实后端与完整追踪，强化对合规性和自主能力的衡量，为后续扩展至更多场景提供基础。

Abstract: Production voice agents span cascaded, speech-to-speech, and hybrid architectures. Voice-agent benchmarks typically measure component quality and conversational properties such as word error rate, latency, naturalness, and turn-taking. Fewer measure whether the agent handled a phone call correctly on its own. Contact centers refer to this as ``containment'': the share of phone calls the automated system resolves without handing off to a human. On some phone calls the right outcome is refusal or a redirect. To address this gap, we introduce VAmoS Bench, the Voice Agent Simulation Bench. It measures complete voice-agent systems end to end in a stateful customer-support task. The agent is Riley, a credit-card support representative for a fictional bank who can freeze, cancel, replace, or activate a card. Each of 100 scenarios supplies a simulated caller with a private goal and a seeded PostgreSQL backend. The platform uses each scenario to populate and activate an isolated simulation in which the caller reaches Riley over audio; roughly one-third apply adversarial pressure. The agent can use five tools that execute real SQL against the backend. Each scenario also defines binary assertions. A grader evaluates them against the complete trace of what the caller and agent said and what the agent did, including tool invocations, arguments, and returned rows. This catches an agent that claims to have changed a card without updating the database, as well as one that makes the right database change while disclosing protected information. This first benchmark version focuses on financial services. Its evaluation protocol supports an evolving leaderboard: additional voice agents can be evaluated on the same version, while later versions can expand the tasks and scenarios.

</details>


### [227] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究面向多模态智能体的音频提示注入攻击，提出指令增强与场景隐藏技术实现隐蔽攻击，并构建首个基准AudioAgentSecurity，评估11个先进智能体（平均攻击成功率69.10%），同时提出基于声源分离与一致性分析的防御机制CADV（检测成功率超90%），并通过真实场景实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体通过连续音频交互执行任务时，音频输入不可避免包含环境噪声，形成未被充分研究的攻击面；现有研究缺乏对音频提示注入攻击的系统性量化与防御。

Method: 提出指令增强与场景隐藏两种新技术，使恶意音频指令以感知不可察觉的方式叠加到用户语音中；构建包含8个真实任务场景和10种攻击模式的基准AudioAgentSecurity；设计基于声源分离与跨模态一致性分析的防御机制CADV。

Result: 在Gemini 3 Pro上平均攻击成功率达69.10%；CADV对多种攻击向量的检测成功率超过90%；真实场景（Doubao AI Smartphone）实验证实攻击的高隐蔽性与有效性，且防御可靠。

Conclusion: 音频提示注入对多模态智能体构成严重现实威胁，所提攻击方法具有高隐蔽性和有效性，而基于声学的CADV防御能显著提升安全性，为后续研究提供基准与防护方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [228] [The Convergence Behavior of Adam under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27383)
*Yijiang Pang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 首次为向量形式Adam在重尾随机噪声下建立收敛保证，发现其迭代复杂度次优且依赖p，但在已知域半径时可达最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Adam变体在bounded-variance非凸优化中已达最优，但缺乏对仅具有有界p阶中心矩（p∈(1,2]）的重尾噪声场景的理论理解，而该场景在现代深度学习中日益普遍。

Method: 将在线到非凸转换框架推广到重尾鞅差噪声，并基于此对Adam进行折扣遗憾分析，避免参数耦合限制。

Result: Adam在重尾噪声下收敛到(ρ,ε)-平稳点，但迭代复杂度次优且收敛速率依赖p，即使p=2时次优性仍存在；当利用已知域半径控制在线学习器输出时，复杂度改善至最优。

Conclusion: 揭示了Adam在重尾场景下的鲁棒性与局限性，表明其标准形式次优，但通过域半径信息可恢复最优收敛率。

Abstract: We establish the first convergence guarantees for the plain vector-form \emph{Adam} optimizer under heavy-tailed stochastic noise. While several Adam variants are known to achieve optimal iteration complexity in bounded-variance nonconvex optimization, little is understood about their behavior when stochastic gradients admit only a bounded $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1,2]$, a setting increasingly observed in modern deep learning. To address this gap, we generalize the recent online-to-nonconvex conversion framework to accommodate heavy-tailed martingale-difference noise. Building on this generalized framework, we develop a discounted regret analysis for Adam, without restrictive parameter coupling. Our results show that Adam converges to $(ρ,ε)$-stationary points under heavy-tailed noise. However, it exhibits a suboptimal iteration complexity and $p$-dependent convergence, a suboptimality that persists even in the bounded-variance case ($p=2$). When the domain radius is known and used to control the online-learner output, a standard setup in related literature, the convergence rate improves to match the optimal complexity. These findings provide new theoretical insight into the robustness and limitations of Adam in heavy-tailed regimes.

</details>


### [229] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了世界模型在具身AI中的安全威胁，提出覆盖全生命周期的攻击分类法，并探讨其作为安全盾牌与预测幻觉的双重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为具身AI提供预测核心，但这一预测层引入了新的安全边界——数据、传感器、提示或反馈的篡改可能传播至物理动作。现有研究孤立看待世界模型，缺乏对其全生命周期威胁的系统分析。

Method: 构建生命周期分类法，从数据构建、表征学习、状态接地、想象、轨迹评估、执行到长期适应（记忆与工具）追踪威胁；映射各攻击家族（投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵、供应链攻击）到世界模型的安全属性（状态、动力学、可供性、安全成本），并提出评估协议与结构化防御框架。

Result: 展示了熟悉攻击在世界模型语境下的新含义，揭示了世界模型作为运行时安全防护与受损时产生预测安全幻觉的双重性；提供了安全失败评估协议及跨来源、鲁棒接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障的防御体系。

Conclusion: 世界模型的安全需从全生命周期视角审视，其既是防护工具也是潜在攻击面，需通过多维度防御与审计确保预测可靠性，避免过度信任导致的安全幻觉。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


### [230] [FunL2O: LLM-Guided Feature Function Design for Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27389)
*Bingheng Li,Junyang Cai,Yupeng Zhang,Bistra Dilkina,Jayant Kalagnanam,Dzung T. Phan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出FunL2O，首个用LLM驱动程序演化自动设计L2O特征函数的统一框架，在连续与离散优化任务中超越手工特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有L2O方法依赖手工设计的特征函数，导致表示设计需手动且跨领域固定，缺乏自动化与泛化性。

Method: 采用FunSearch式循环：LLM迭代生成可执行特征函数，固定评估流程重训原L2O模型并依据下游优化性能筛选，实现特征自动演化。

Result: 在LP/QP的解决方案预测与热启动、MIP的GNN后门分支与预测搜索任务中，四种LLM生成的特征均一致优于手工表示。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的特征演化可作为L2O中表示设计的通用有效自动化方法。

Abstract: Learning-to-optimize (L2O) methods accelerate repeated optimization by training models to predict solutions, warm starts, branching decisions, or other forms of solver guidance. A critical yet largely overlooked component of these pipelines is the feature function that maps problem instances to inputs for machine learning models. Existing L2O methods typically rely on hand-crafted features, making representation design manual and largely fixed across domains. We introduce FunL2O, the first unified framework for automating feature design through LLM-driven program evolution for L2O. In a FunSearch-style loop, an LLM proposes executable feature functions, while a fixed evaluation process retrains the original L2O model and measures downstream optimization performance. We evaluate FunL2O on linear and quadratic programming tasks involving solution prediction and warm-starting, as well as on mixed-integer optimization tasks using GNN-guided backdoor branching and Predict-and-Search. Across continuous and discrete optimization tasks and four LLMs, the evolved features consistently outperform hand-crafted representations. These results establish LLM-driven feature evolution as a general and effective approach to automating representation design in L2O.

</details>


### [231] [A dataset of rated conceptual arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27499)
*Emery Cooper,Caspar Oesterheld,Linh Chi Nguyen,Alexander Kastner,Ethan Perez*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含951条论辩性评论和442篇立场文本的数据集，并基于专家评分基准测试了多种模型在概念性问题上推理能力，发现模型表现与通用能力排名相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在可验证任务上进步迅速，但其对缺乏客观答案、依赖辩论论证的概念性问题的推理能力尚不清楚，此类问题广泛存在于哲学、AI安全、决策理论和社会选择等领域。

Method: 构建数据集，由六位专家按中心性、强度、正确性和清晰度四个维度对论辩性评论进行评分，提出两种评分函数，并基准测试多款模型。

Result: 模型性能与通用能力排名保持一致，表明当前模型在概念性推理上仍受整体能力制约。

Conclusion: 概念性问题的评估应聚焦于具体论点的质量而非最终结论，模型在该任务上的表现可作为能力诊断工具，但需进一步改进以支持可靠推理。

Abstract: Large language models have improved rapidly on tasks with verifiable answers, such as mathematics and programming. Much less is known about their ability to reason about what we call conceptual questions: questions for which no ground truth is realistically accessible and no widely accepted resolution methodology exists, but on which progress can still be made by debating arguments. Most philosophical questions are of this kind, as are central components of questions in AI safety, decision theory, and social choice. Our approach is based on the view that while bottom-line conclusions on such questions are hard to evaluate, individual contextualized arguments can be evaluated far more reliably. We therefore introduce a dataset of 951 argumentative critiques of 442 position texts, spanning topics from AI safety and decision theory to ethics and politics, with 1,458 ratings by six expert raters along dimensions including centrality, strength, correctness, and clarity. We propose two scoring functions and benchmark a range of models. Performance tracks general capability rankings.

</details>


### [232] [Formalization of security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28551)
*Gilles Barthe*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本章综述了证明助手在系统安全、语言安全、安全编译和密码学等领域的应用，并强调其支持认证的额外价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明助手常用于验证设计与实现是否满足预期安全属性，并支持安全认证流程，因此有必要系统梳理其在安全相关方向的应用。

Method: 按四个安全领域分别阐述证明助手的具体应用方式，包括系统安全、基于语言的安全、安全编译以及密码学验证。

Result: 展示了证明助手在上述安全子领域中的验证能力与适用场景，并指出其对认证工作的支撑作用。

Conclusion: 证明助手是安全验证与认证的重要工具，可在多个安全层面提供形式化保证。

Abstract: Proof assistants are often used to validate that designs and implementations meet their expected security properties. A further motivation for using proof assistants is to support certification. This chapter focuses on their applications to system security, language-based security, secure compilation, and cryptography.

</details>


### [233] [Context-Informed Ship Trajectory Prediction via Conditional Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27418)
*Yuan Guan,Chandler Squires,Timothy Hu,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出条件信息器（Conditional Informer），将船舶轨迹预测建模为条件生成任务，通过条件注意力机制显式编码环境对船舶运动的单向物理依赖，并引入模态掩码训练策略应对传感器缺失场景，在AIS和ERA5数据上显著提升预测精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer轨迹预测模型主要依赖历史运动状态，忽略外部环境（如天气）与静态船舶特性的影响；多模态方法将环境变量作为平行特征，未编码环境对船舶运动的因果方向性，导致预测准确性受限。

Method: 提出Conditional Informer编码器-解码器架构，采用条件注意力机制，使船舶状态作为查询项通过交叉注意力显式查询环境上下文，编码“天气调节船舶运动但不受船舶运动影响”的物理先验；同时设计模态掩码训练策略，随机屏蔽部分输入模态以模拟传感器失效，防止捷径学习并提升数据间断时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在AIS与ERA5数据上，当环境上下文可用时，所提方法预测精度较运动学与拼接基线提升15.4%；模态掩码训练将传感器降级时的预测误差降低近一个数量级，有效避免捷径学习。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了将环境作为条件而非平等特征的有效性，并证明模态掩码能显著增强模型在真实数据缺失场景下的可靠性，为船舶长时轨迹预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Long-term ship trajectory prediction is a fundamental capability for maritime safety and autonomous navigation. While recent Transformer-based architectures have improved forecasting horizons, they predominantly rely on historical kinematic states, treating vessel motion as an isolated system. In reality, maritime navigation is profoundly modulated by extrinsic factors like weather and constrained by static vessel characteristics. Existing multimodal approaches fundamentally model the joint distribution over states and contexts, treating environmental variables as peer features rather than encoding the directional physical dependence of vessel dynamics on environmental conditions.
  In this work, we propose the Conditional Informer, a novel encoder-decoder architecture that formulates trajectory prediction as a conditional generation task. We employ a dedicated Conditional Attention mechanism where the vessel state explicitly queries environmental contexts through cross-attention, encoding the physical prior that weather modulates - but is not generated by - vessel dynamics. Furthermore, to address the intermittency of real-world data, we introduce a Modality Masking training strategy to prevent catastrophic degradation during sensor fallback. Extensive experiments on AIS and ERA5 data demonstrate that our approach outperforms kinematic and concatenation-based baselines by 15.4% in prediction accuracy when context is available. Crucially, Modality Masking prevents shortcut learning, reducing fallback error by nearly an order of magnitude compared to unconstrained models.

</details>


### [234] [Automated Transcript Analysis for Detecting Flaws in Agentic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27518)
*Jeff Mohl,Nelson Gardner-Challis,Magda Dubois,Harry Coppock,Benjamin Allan-Rahill,Kaelan Yim,Damian Sójka,James Mann,Justin Olive*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文开发了AI扫描器，用于自动检测智能体基准测试中的四类有效性问题，并在多个广泛使用的基准上发现若干问题，证明自动化审计基准质量的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工审计基准（如SWE-Bench-Verified）虽有效但难以规模化，且不确定自动化方法能否可靠发现损害基准有效性的缺陷。

Method: 设计四种AI扫描器分别检测ground truth访问、工具故障、猜测脆弱性和答案格式歧义；为每种问题制定评分标准指导人工标注，并在Inspect Evals基准的留出测试集上对比扫描器与人工标注结果。

Result: 扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准中发现多个已验证的质量问题，部分难以通过随机人工检查发现；但检出率因基准、标准和模型而异，未覆盖全部案例，且性能受评估领域标准化缺口影响。

Conclusion: 该研究作为概念验证，表明自动化转录分析可扩展用于基准质量审计，但需解决标准化等开放挑战以增强质量保证的可靠性。

Abstract: Capabilities of frontier models are often assessed using agentic benchmarks. To trust these results, benchmarks must accurately measure what they claim to and be free from invalidating flaws. Previous manual audits of benchmarks such as SWE-Bench-Verified have uncovered several validity issues in transcripts. However, manual review is difficult to scale, and it is unclear whether automated methods can reliably surface flaws that compromise benchmark validity. In this paper, we developed AI scanners to detect four types of validity issues: ground truth access, tool failure, guessing vulnerability, and answer format ambiguity. We produced grading rubrics for each to instruct human labeling, and evaluated the scanners against human labels on a held-out test set of Inspect Evals benchmarks. Our scanners identified several verified quality issues in five widely used benchmarks, including cases unlikely to be caught by random manual inspection. Not all cases were identified, and scanner performance varied substantially across benchmarks, criteria and models. We highlight several open challenges to be addressed to improve scanners for stronger quality assurance claims, including broader standardization gaps in the evaluation field that degrade scanner performance. Together, these results serve as a proof of concept for using automated transcript analysis to audit benchmark quality more broadly.

</details>


### [235] [Old Tricks, New Models: How Simple Image Transformations Break Modern AI-based Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28187)
*Marco Alecci,Francesco Marchiori,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Jacques Klein*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文通过黑盒测试发现，三家商业多模态审核API在简单图像变换下均可被绕过，且鲁棒性差异显著，表明基础模型API不能单独作为安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审核系统依赖任务特定分类器，策略覆盖和上下文理解有限；新兴的基于大模型的商业API声称提供更广泛的安全过滤，但作者质疑其实际鲁棒性是否真的更优。

Method: 对三家商业图像审核服务进行大规模黑盒评估，采用七种简单、模型无关的图像变换，并跨多个提供商、数据集、危害类别、感知相似性约束和变换强度进行测试。

Result: 所有三家服务都能被无梯度、无替代模型、无目标系统知识的廉价变换绕过；固定变换（如颜色反转和灰度化）可导致不安全到安全的决策改变，同时内容对人类仍可识别；鲁棒性在数据集和危害类别间差异大，多模态内容和自残类别漏洞最明显。

Conclusion: 用基础模型API替换传统审核分类器不能自动形成可靠安全边界；此类系统需在真实变换下评估，并作为分层审核管道的一部分而非独立过滤器部署。

Abstract: While automated content-moderation systems have become essential for screening harmful content at scale, conventional task-specific classifiers often provide limited policy cov- erage and contextual understanding. Recently, commercial multimodal moderation APIs built on large foundation models have been introduced with the promise of providing broader and more capable safety filters. In this work, we analyze whether this shift also yields more robust image moderation. We conduct a large-scale black-box evaluation on three established commercial image-moderation services and compare their robustness. By evaluating seven simple, model-agnostic image transformations across multiple providers, datasets, harm categories, perceptual-similarity constraints, and transformation intensities, we find that: (1) all three commercial services can be bypassed using inexpensive image transformations that require no gradients, surrogate models, or knowledge of the target system; (2) even fixed transformations such as color inversion and grayscale conversion induce unsafe-to-safe decision changes while preserving content that remains recognizable to humans; (3) their robustness varies substantially across datasets and harm categories, with multimodal content and self-harm exhibiting pronounced vulnerabilities. This yields the conclusion that replacing conventional moderation classifiers with foundation-model-based APIs does not, by itself, provide a reliable security boundary. Such systems must be evaluated under realistic transformations and deployed as one component of a layered moderation pipeline rather than as standalone safety filters.

</details>


### [236] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 针对安全运营中心告警疲劳问题，提出一种结合思维链推理、自训练与可验证奖励强化学习的告警分类系统，并设计校准器修正推理导致的标签概率失真，在真实端点检测数据上达到82.6%准确率，高置信度场景下良性与恶意告警召回率分别提升43.0%和18.3%，且30B微调模型优于通用前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅让大语言模型直接输出告警分类标签，未训练其推理检测是否为真实威胁；同时告警数量远超分析师处理能力，导致告警疲劳，亟需提升分类准确性及可信度。

Method: 在真实人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上，结合自动提示优化、自训练和基于可验证奖励的强化学习训练思维链推理分类器；另训练独立校准器读取完整推理轨迹，估计分类正确概率，解决推理导致标签概率退化的问题。

Result: 测试准确率82.6%，高置信度工作点下良性召回率提升43.0%、恶意召回率提升18.3%，优于直接标签分类器；未训练校准器会使高置信度召回率降为零，30B微调模型效果显著优于通用大模型。

Conclusion: 针对安全领域定向训练优于单纯扩大模型规模，且推理与概率校准结合可显著提升告警分类的可靠性和可操作性，为SOC自动化分流提供有效方案。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [237] [Subtract or Replay? Exact Deletion from Language-Model Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27539)
*Vishwajith Ramesh*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 精确删除持久化语言模型记忆取决于记忆如何表示记录：可寻址影响可通过代数递减删除，而共享循环状态中被后期写入转化的影响需要从写入前的状态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法缺乏精确性保证，特别是在模型记忆由不同结构（如全局注意力、循环状态）表示时，删除效果不一致且无法达到与从未摄入等同的水平。

Method: 在Gemma 3中用支持向量记忆替换全局注意力层，测试低秩恢复后的递减与保留键重拟合；在Kimi Linear混合模型中区分加性写入、对角衰减和delta规则，并实现检查点回退重放以删除真实临床记录。

Result: Gemma 3在1B规模下递减与重拟合的KL散度中位数为5.4e-15，困惑度相对微调增加2.0%；4B和12B下效用成本升至11.2%和44.3%。Kimi Linear中delta规则导致12%-49%贡献后缀依赖，重放删除在18,842令牌上下文中与从未摄入logits和状态完全一致。

Conclusion: 精确删除是记忆表示的性质：可寻址记录用递减，纠缠写入用重放，从而在确定性实现中达到位级一致的删除与修正。

Abstract: Exact deletion from persistent language-model memory depends on how that memory represents a record. Addressable influence can be removed by algebraic decrement; influence transformed by later writes inside shared recurrent state requires rebuilding from before the write. We test this distinction in two pretrained models against explicit record-omitted references. First, we replace Gemma 3's global-attention layers with support-vector memory. After low-rank recovery at 1B, decrement and retained-key refit agree at the next-token output to median KL $5.4\times10^{-15}$ over 31 support-token deletions, with $+2.0\%$ perplexity relative to a matched fine-tune. A masked-refit proxy is indistinguishable from the never-ingested floor under elicitation, relearning, sampling, and LiRA attacks. At 4B and 12B, certificate ordering persists but utility cost rises to $11.2\%$ and $44.3\%$. Second, in a 48B Kimi Linear hybrid, additive writes admit a fixed decrement and diagonal decay a corrected one, whereas the delta rule makes $12$--$49\%$ of a record's contribution suffix-dependent. Checkpointed rewind-and-replay deletes real clinical records at contexts up to 18,842 tokens, matching never-ingested logits and all recurrent states bit for bit within a deterministic MLX implementation; replaying a correction provides exact amendment. Exact deletion is therefore a property of memory representation: subtract addressable records and replay entangled writes.

</details>


### [238] [Compliance2LoRA: On-Demand Safety Alignment on Arbitrary Policy Subsets via Hypernetwork-Generated LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27594)
*Pankayaraj Pathmanathan,Furong Huang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出统一的自适应超网络框架，将安全策略作为可定制输入，生成LoRA权重，实现单一大型推理模型按需调整策略合规性，避免组合开销和长上下文计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型个性化部署要求适配不同策略子集，单独训练每个子集产生严重组合开销，而上下文学习方法虽避免此问题但引入长上下文生成的计算挑战。

Method: 采用超网络作为LoRA适配器生成器，将安全策略编码为输入，学习生成对应策略合规的LoRA权重，添加到下游模型后实现按需策略调整。

Result: 在多种规模推理模型和不同评测数据集上，证明该超网络训练能在不牺牲任务性能的前提下实现单模型按需策略调整。

Conclusion: 自适应超网络对齐方法在大型推理模型中有效且实用，为多策略合规提供轻量级统一解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training alignment in large reasoning models (LRMs) has significantly improved their adaptability to diverse safety compliance settings. However, as LRMs personalization for downstream users takes center stage, the demand for varying levels of policy compliance grows as different user-specific LRMs must adhere to distinct subsets of safety policies. Training a separate LRM for each policy subset introduces severe combinatorial overhead. While in context learning methods overcome this combinatorial overhead, they introduce additional computational challenges associated with long context generation. To address this challenge, we propose \ours, a unified adaptive hypernetwork-based framework for multi-policy compliance. In our framework, safety policies serve as customizable inputs to a LoRA adapter generator, which learns to produce policy compliant LoRA weights for downstream LRM. When added to the LRM these weights enable the generation of responses compliant with the specified policy subsets. In this work, we demonstrate that training such a hypernetwork enables on-demand policy adjustments on a single LRM without sacrificing task performance across reasoning models of different sized and different evaluation datasets. This highlights the effectiveness and practicality of adaptive hypernetwork based alignment in LRMs.

</details>


### [239] [Real-Time Hard Peak Age-of-Information Safety with No-Regret Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27626)
*Wentao Zhang,Wentao Mo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出OCO-PAoI-Hard框架，将Io T硬实时峰值信息年龄调度转化为时变约束在线凸优化，通过单次欧氏投影保证每时隙零违规，并实现O(sqrt(T))遗憾界与匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在对抗性信道或缺乏精确系统模型时无法保证硬截止期限的零违规，而峰值AoI约束可精确表示为仿射半空间约束，从而建立凸优化与实时调度的新联系。

Method: 将分数化峰值AoI截止期限转化为资源分配向量的仿射半空间约束，采用严格因果的提议-屏蔽-更新循环，每时隙执行一次欧氏投影保证可行性，梯度步保持无遗憾性，虚拟队列仅作为事后证书，并给出静态/动态遗憾的闭式界和最小最大下界。

Result: 在四传感器对抗性流体模型陷阱信道上，OCO-PAoI-Hard在十个随机种子下均实现零建模状态违规，而四个基线错过1.65%至64.0%的时隙；经验归一化遗憾在两个数量级的T内低于理论包络。

Conclusion: 该框架为对抗性环境下的硬实时Io T调度提供了首个基于在线凸优化的零违规方案，兼具理论保证与实证有效性，并建议在更高维度和真实网络栈中进一步验证。

Abstract: Safety-critical IoT systems such as industrial closed-loop control, V2X coordination, and remote teleoperation require every sensor's peak Age of Information (peak AoI, also abbreviated PAoI) to stay below a hard per-slot deadline, not merely an average bound. Existing approaches meet this requirement only under restrictive assumptions: stochastic channels for Whittle-index AoI, simulator rollouts for deep reinforcement learning, or sublinear cumulative violation for long-term constrained online convex optimization. Under adversarial coefficients, OCO-PAoI-Hard guarantees zero per-slot violation of the modeled AoI state under one-step viability and O(sqrt(T)) regret against any static safe comparator; packet-level safety requires stronger service assumptions. Our key observation is that the fractional peak-AoI deadline collapses exactly to an affine half-space constraint on the resource-allocation vector, turning hard real-time scheduling into time-varying constrained online convex optimization over a polyhedral safe set. A strictly causal proposal-shield-update loop enforces feasibility through one Euclidean projection per slot, the gradient step preserves no-regret behavior, and the classical virtual queue is reduced to an a-posteriori certificate. We establish closed-form static and dynamic regret bounds, a matching Omega(sqrt(T)) minimax lower bound, a margin-safe variant against execution noise, and a deadline-induced competitive ratio. On a four-sensor adversarial fluid-model trap channel, OCO-PAoI-Hard attains zero modeled-state deadline violations across all ten seeds, while four representative baselines miss between 1.65 percent and 64.0 percent of slots, and the empirical normalized regret stays below the theoretical envelope across two orders of magnitude in T.

</details>


### [240] [First-order Constrained Trilevel Optimization Over Distributed Networks for Robust Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27632)
*Yang Jiao,Kaixuan Jiao,Kai Yang,Nadjib Aitsaadi,Ilhem Fajjari,Renwei,Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出F²CTO，首个面向分布式鲁棒核心集选择的三层优化求解方法，实现非渐近ε-平稳点收敛率O(ε^{-3/2})，并在持续学习任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网边缘网络产生海量数据，全量训练带来计算与存储瓶颈，核心集选择成为关键；同时本地数据隐私敏感且需模型鲁棒性，但分布式鲁棒核心集选择尚无有效框架。

Method: 将问题建模为带层级约束的三层优化，提出F²CTO算法，结合层级复合值函数重构与分布式交替投影梯度下降，实现分布式求解。

Result: 理论证明收敛率O(ε^{-3/2})，实验在可靠持续学习任务上验证了方法的有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: F²CTO填补了分布式鲁棒核心集选择空白，并首次为带层级约束的三层优化提供分布式解法，为边缘智能提供可行方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of the Internet of Things (IoT), massive amounts of data are generated across distributed edge networks. Training models on full data incurs significant computational overhead and storage bottlenecks, rendering coreset selection a critical paradigm. Furthermore, given the privacy-sensitive nature of local data and the escalating demand for model robustness in real-world deployments, developing an effective distributed optimization framework for robust coreset selection is vital, yet remains largely unexplored. To this end, this work first characterizes the hierarchical dependencies among coreset selection, robust optimization, and distributed learning, and formulates the distributed robust coreset selection as a trilevel optimization problem with level-wise constraints. Furthermore, to effectively solve the trilevel problem in a distributed manner, the \underline{F}ederated \underline{F}irst-order \underline{C}onstrained \underline{T}rilevel \underline{O}ptimization (F$^2$CTO) is proposed, which synergistically integrates a hierarchical composite value-function reformulation and a distributed alternating projected gradient algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, F$^2$CTO is the first method developed for distributed robust coreset selection, as well as the first distributed optimization approach for trilevel optimization problems with level-wise constraints. Additionally, we prove that the proposed method achieves a non-asymptotic convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3/2})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary point. Extensive empirical evaluations on reliable continual learning demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed F$^2$CTO.

</details>


### [241] [Looped Transformers with Source-Centered State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27656)
*Bum Jun Kim,Kohei Hayashi,Shunsuke Kamiya,Masanori Koyama,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出源中心状态演化（SCSE）以调和循环Transformer中的输入条件与参考保持共享循环，通过零偏差掩码保证锚点精确不变，并在多个语言建模基准上提升受控循环质量前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过固定参数增加有效深度，但共享块需管理整个训练与外推深度上的隐藏状态轨迹；在加法注入循环中，输入条件信号每步重新注入，导致共享转移无法保持参考状态不变，需设计兼顾输入依赖与参考保持的循环机制。

Method: SCSE通过学习锚点与初始偏差保留输入依赖，允许非零偏差驱动循环计算并将零偏差映射为零，利用零偏差掩码实现精确锚点不变性；将锚点设为单步不动点，零偏差强制偏置由锚点自身产生并在SCSE中消失，非零偏差保持活跃以支持状态相关循环。

Result: 在WikiText-2、WikiText-103、直接网页语料预训练、留出网页文本迁移及LAMBADA补全任务上，SCSE一致提升受控循环质量前沿；消融研究显示学习锚点与锚坐标偏差循环是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: 零偏差强制偏置是可设计自由度，其任务效应或有害或中性或有益，SCSE通过置零偏置选择精确锚点不变性；案例研究将锚点响应诊断与观测循环运动联系起来，验证方法有效性。

Abstract: Looped Transformers create a useful train- and test-time compute axis by reusing the same Transformer block over recurrent depth, increasing effective depth at a fixed parameter count. However, that shared block must then govern an entire trajectory of varying hidden states over trained and extrapolated depths. Furthermore, in additive-injection looped Transformers, an input-conditioned signal is reintroduced at every recurrent step, so applying the shared transition at an input-conditioned reference can still move the hidden state. In this paper, we propose Source-Centered State Evolution (SCSE), which is designed to reconcile input conditioning with reference-preserving shared recurrence. Specifically, SCSE retains input dependence through its learned anchor and initial deviation, allows nonzero deviations to drive recurrent computation while mapping zero deviation to zero, and guarantees exact anchor invariance through its zero-deviation mask. The designated anchor is thereby a one-step fixed point by construction. The zero-deviation forcing bias is the next deviation produced from the anchor itself and vanishes in SCSE, while nonzero deviations remain active and support state-dependent recurrent computation. Our theory shows that the zero-deviation forcing bias is a design degree of freedom whose task effect can be harmful, neutral, or beneficial; SCSE resolves this choice in favor of exact anchor invariance by setting the bias to zero. Across WikiText-2, WikiText-103, direct web-corpus pretraining, held-out web-text transfer, and LAMBADA completion, SCSE improves the controlled recurrent quality frontier. Ablation studies identify the learned anchor and the anchor-coordinate deviation recurrence as the primary contributors to the gain, and a trained-model case study grounds the anchor-response diagnostic in observed recurrent motion.

</details>


### [242] [Crossing the Margin Cliff: Toward Relearn-Robust LLM Unlearning via Margin Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27836)
*Xiangyu Yin,Jiaxu Liu,Zhen Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 大语言模型遗忘在再学习攻击下普遍脆弱，本文发现并证明‘边际悬崖’现象，提出边际校准（MC）方法，在多种模型与数据集上显著提升遗忘鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法在再学习攻击下极易失效，简单微调即可恢复遗忘知识，缺乏理论解释与通用解法。

Method: 提出边际校准（MC），结合非饱和边际铰链损失与KL探针，并利用梯度主导条件分析其优化几何。

Result: 在TOFU、MUSE-News、Phi-3.5等数据集上，单一配置赢下全部14项头对头遗忘汇总与所有再学习单元，攻击后ROUGE-L从0.41降至0.18，并降低13/14的成员推理AUC。

Conclusion: MC作为即插即用方法显著提升遗忘鲁棒性，主要代价是保留侧效用下降，并提供部署变体无需保留参考。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning is consistently fragile under relearn attacks. On TOFU, fine-tuning on twenty forget examples substantially recovers held-out forget-set ROUGE for every method we evaluate, and we trace this fragility to optimization geometry. The per-token answer margin of fourteen post-hoc methods spanning gradient, preference, and distillation families converges into a narrow band above the retain reference in 41 of 42 method--size cells, a regularity we call the margin cliff. We prove that this cliff follows whenever the retain coupling holds the diagnostic log-odds of forget content above a floor, a condition that token-saturating losses induce at stationarity and that we verify directly on 34 of 42 cells. Margin Calibration (\textsc{MC}) is a plug-in polish adding a non-saturating margin hinge anchored at the reference's per-token margin plus a KL probe on a disjoint instruction corpus, restoring forget-side pressure where the native loss saturates. Under a stated gradient-dominance condition, whose on-trajectory gradient signature we measure by instrumenting the polish, its stationary set lies on the cliff-crossing side, yielding an attack-budget upper bound on the relearn margin lift. Across TOFU (three Llama-3 sizes, three forget tiers), MUSE-News on Llama-2-7B-hf, and a Phi-3.5 panel, a single frozen configuration wins all 14 head-to-head forget aggregates and all populated relearn cells (panel-mean post-attack ROUGE-L $0.41$ to $0.18$) and lowers raw membership AUC on 13/14, with reduced retain-side utility as the main cost. A deployment variant matches these gains without a retain-trained reference.

</details>


### [243] [VESTIGE: A Knowledge-Guided Masking Strategy for Corruption-Aware Fine-Tuning of Genomic Transformers, Validated on Ancient DNA Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27712)
*Angshuman Chakravertty,Rahul Maheshwari*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出VESTIGE，一种参数无关的掩码语言模型训练方法，按经验测量的每位置损伤分布进行掩码，在古DNA重建中显著优于标准MLM，且适用于多种退化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLM均匀掩码假设各位置重建难度相同，但在损伤集中且可预测的位置失效，峰值损伤处模型甚至不如随机预测器。

Method: VESTIGE以mapDamage2得到的每位置脱氨损伤剖面为掩码分布，保持平均C/G掩码率15%与标准MLM一致，仅改变空间分布。

Result: 在猛犸象CDS数据集上，VESTIGE在六个末端区域宽度下均优于标准MLM（Δ+4.18至+10.35个百分点），验证交叉熵降低13%，ESMFold重建TM-score均>0.95，生物安全分类器AUC=0.935且98.2%窗口通过。

Conclusion: 原理可推广至任何可测量位置或上下文相关损伤场景，VESTIGE为退化序列输入提供知识引导的训练方法。

Abstract: Standard masked-language-model fine-tuning applies a uniform masking probability across every token position, assuming reconstruction difficulty is position-agnostic. When the degradation process is characterised and concentrated at predictable positions, this assumption fails: at peak damage sites the model can underperform a frequency-matched random predictor. We introduce VESTIGE, a parameter-free, drop-in replacement for the standard MLM collator that aligns the masking distribution with an empirically measured per-position corruption profile. We apply it to ancient DNA (aDNA) reconstruction, where cytosine deamination produces a position-dependent C-to-T / G-to-A gradient quantified per-position by mapDamage2. Rescaling so the mean C/G masking rate equals 15% - identical to standard MLM - isolates spatial redistribution as the sole variable, with model, data, seed, and hyperparameters held fixed across both DNABERT-2 runs on a mammoth CDS corpus (two specimens, seven genes). Across six terminal-zone widths and 626 paired windows, VESTIGE leads standard MLM at every width (Delta = +4.18 to +10.35 pp, all p < 10^-8), cuts validation cross-entropy by 13% (3.274 vs. 3.757), and yields ESMFold reconstructions with TM-score > 0.95 across all six reconstructions (three genes) even under damage amplified 10-30x beyond authentic PMD rates. A 1D CNN biosecurity classifier returns AUC = 0.935 and clears 98.2% of reconstructed windows, the 1.76% remainder attributable to reference-genome features, not reconstruction artefacts. The principle is domain-agnostic: any measurable position- or context-specific corruption profile - FFPE, bisulfite, metagenomic, or nanopore - substitutes directly for the PMD array, making VESTIGE a knowledge-guided training routine for intelligent systems operating on degraded or noisy sequence inputs.

</details>


### [244] [Improving the Robustness/Accuracy Tradeoff Against Adversarial Attacks Using Information Bottleneck Distillation Through Dual Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27737)
*Vincent Ryusuke Takahashi,Yoshinari Takeishi,Jun'ichi Takeuchi,Kave Salamatian*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种双教师知识蒸馏方法，在IBD框架中引入干净教师模型，通过跨层注意力矩阵融合干净与鲁棒特征，在保持对抗鲁棒性的同时提升干净样本准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IBD方法在提升对抗鲁棒性的同时牺牲了干净样本的分类精度，需要一种既能保持鲁棒性又能恢复干净精度的方法。

Method: 在IBD基础上增加一个仅用干净数据训练的教师模型（干净教师），与对抗训练的鲁棒教师共同蒸馏给学生网络，通过跨层注意力矩阵实现两个教师特征的融合传递。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，相比IBD，干净样本精度提升且对抗精度基本保持；与B-MTARD等SOTA方法相比，在干净与鲁棒准确率的调和平均数上具有竞争力；同时分析了不同训练设置对注意力模块的影响。

Conclusion: 双教师蒸馏策略能有效平衡干净精度与鲁棒性，且该方法在性能上达到当前先进水平，为后续鲁棒蒸馏研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have achieved remarkable success in classical machine learning problems. However, they are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Countermeasures proposed in the literature, notably Information Bottleneck Distillation (IBD) introduced by Kuang et al., degrade the classification accuracy on clean inputs while improving the robustness to adversarial inputs. In this work, we extend the IBD framework by introducing an extra teacher model (clean teacher) trained with only clean inputs, into the distillation process from a robust teacher model trained by adversarial training. The features of both clean and robust teachers are transferred to the student through a cross-layer attention matrix. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 datasets show that the proposed method improves classification accuracy on clean samples compared to the original IBD, while maintaining similar accuracy on adversarial samples. Furthermore, our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art approaches, including the recent dual-teacher distillation framework B-MTARD, particularly in terms of the harmonic mean between clean and robust accuracy. We also analyze the impact of different training settings that have different influences on the attention module.

</details>


### [245] [A Cross-Architecture Audit of Direction-Based Inference-Time Defences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27910)
*Xiangyu Yin,Tora Bodin,Rohan Menon,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 在受控幅度协议下比较五种视觉语言模型越狱防御方向，发现无单一方法在所有模型上占优，图像条件偏移在多数架构中表现最佳且效用损失最低，但防御方向具有架构特异性，需按模型族单独校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时防御常通过从残差流中减去校准方向来抵抗视觉语言模型越狱，但现有研究缺乏对多种防御方向的系统比较，且未充分考虑干预幅度控制和方向特异性的影响。

Method: 采用幅度受控协议，匹配每提示的干预大小，并配对随机控制方向；比较五种候选防御（均值图像条件偏移、CMRM风格拒绝方向、ShiftDC风格攻击特异残差、提示忽略图像指令、随机控制），覆盖四个架构家族的15个模型-层组合，并分析方向间的余弦对齐与迁移性。

Result: 图像条件偏移在LLaVA 1.5和Pixtral 12B上最优，且唯一在所有家族中效用损失处于测量噪声底；提示指令在Qwen2.5 VL上最优，攻击特异残差在Qwen2 VL 2B上最优；图像条件方向在13/15单元格中具方向特异性，但在LLaVA 1.5 13B与Pixtral 12B间不可迁移；CMRM方向与图像条件偏移在所有15个单元格中正余弦对齐（均值0.35，范围0.17-0.65，为随机向量零分布的15-25倍，符号检验p≈3e-5）。

Conclusion: 两类防御方法恢复部分重叠的几何结构，但方向基防御必须针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准，不存在跨架构通用的最优防御方向。

Abstract: Inference time defences against vision language model jailbreaks often subtract a calibrated direction from the residual stream at a chosen decoder layer. We compare five defence candidates across 15 model and layer cells from four architectural families under a magnitude controlled protocol that matches the intervention size for each prompt and pairs every direction with a random control of the same norm. The candidates are the mean image conditioning shift, a CMRM style refusal direction, a ShiftDC style attack specific residual, a prompt instruction to ignore the image, and a random control. No single candidate dominates on both refusal recovery and utility preservation. The image conditioning shift leads on LLaVA 1.5 and Pixtral 12B and is the only candidate whose utility loss remains at the measurement noise floor in every family. The prompt instruction leads on Qwen2.5 VL, while the attack specific residual leads on Qwen2 VL 2B. The image conditioning direction is direction specific in 13 of 15 cells, but strongly architecture specific and nontransferable across the only dimension compatible pair, LLaVA 1.5 13B and Pixtral 12B. We also connect text only and multimodal refusal geometry. The CMRM direction has positive cosine alignment with the image conditioning shift in all 15 cells, with mean 0.35, range 0.17 to 0.65, 15 to 25 times the random vector null, and a sign test p value of about 3e-5. These results show that the two recipes recover partially overlapping geometry and that direction based defences should be calibrated separately for each language decoder family.

</details>


### [246] [One Anchor for All: Unified Multilingual and Multimodal Safety Alignment for LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27917)
*Enyi Shi,Fei Shen,Chuancheng Shi,Linxia Zhu,Shuyi Miao,Jinhui Tang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模态与语言共享安全神经元（MLS-Neurons）的神经元级跨维度安全对齐框架，仅微调约0.03%参数即可将仅英文安全监督迁移至多语言多模态场景，显著提升复合攻击防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法将语言与模态隔离，且缺乏安全数据与微调成本高，难以应对多语言指令与视觉信息结合的复合攻击。

Method: 通过对比有害与良性样本识别单语单模态安全神经元，跨模态取交集得模态共享神经元（MS-Neurons），再以英文为语义锚跨语言取交集得MLS-Neurons，仅更新该最小共享子集实现安全对齐。

Result: 在多种多语言与多模态安全基准上显著优于现有最优方法，同时保持模型通用能力。

Conclusion: 证明神经元级跨维度共享安全对齐能以极低参数成本高效防御复合攻击，为LVLMs安全部署提供新思路。

Abstract: As large vision-language models (LVLMs) are deployed globally, the combination of multilingual instructions and visual information makes malicious attacks more covert and sophisticated than ever before. However, existing methods isolate language and modality defenses, which, coupled with the scarcity of safety data and high fine-tuning costs, makes it difficult for models to defend against compound attacks. To address this severe challenge, we propose a neuron-level cross-dimensional safety alignment framework driven by modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons). First, we identify monolingual and unimodal safety neurons by comparing responses to harmful and benign samples, quantifying functional saliency through activation strength and downstream impact. Then, by intersecting these unimodal neurons within each language, we extract modality-shared safety neurons (MS-Neurons) responsive to both visual and textual risks, bridging the safety representation gap between modalities. Furthermore, using English as a semantic anchor, we intersect MS-Neurons across languages to identify modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons), serving as key defenses against compound attacks. Finally, we update only this minimal subset of shared neurons (~0.03% of parameters), transferring English-only safety supervision to multilingual and multimodal scenarios. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse multilingual and multimodal safety benchmarks while preserving general utility.

</details>


### [247] [Learning-Augmented and Randomized Algorithms for Line Aggregation with Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27807)
*Tianhang Lu,Runtian Ren,Shengcai Liu,Ke Tang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文针对线度量上的带延迟在线聚合问题，提出学习增强与随机化算法，在鲁棒性和一致性间取得权衡，并改进随机竞争比上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线聚合问题中，传统确定性算法存在竞争比下界4，且缺乏利用预测信息的手段。本文旨在通过引入建议服务长度的在线预测，结合随机化策略，突破确定性下界并提升性能。

Method: 针对每个λ∈(0,1]，设计确定性学习增强Balance算法，通过参数λ平衡鲁棒性和一致性；随后提出经典对抗模型下的随机化算法，利用随机化对抗 oblivious adversary；最后结合两种思想得到随机化学习增强算法。

Result: 确定性算法达到(4/λ+1/λ²)-鲁棒和(4+λ)-一致；随机化算法达到(e+1)-竞争，优于确定性5-竞争下界，且将随机竞争比下界从e/(e-1)提升至e；随机化学习增强算法达到(e/λ+1/λ²)-鲁棒和(e+λ)-一致。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 随机化策略能有效突破确定性下界，学习增强框架能在保证鲁棒性的同时利用预测信息，所提算法在理论上和实证中均优于现有基准。

Abstract: This paper studies learning-augmented and randomized online aggregation with delays on a line metric. We consider advice given as online suggested service lengths, and evaluate the algorithms in terms of robustness and consistency. For each $λ\in (0,1]$, we first propose a deterministic learning-augmented \textsc{Balance} algorithm that is $(4/λ+1/λ^2)$-robust and $(4+λ)$-consistent. We also propose a randomized algorithm for the problem in the classical adversarial model, which is $(e+1)$-competitive against an oblivious adversary, improving over the deterministic $5$-competitive \textsc{Balance} benchmark~\cite{bienkowski2013chain}. Notably, this competitive ratio is even lower than the lower bound of $4$ for deterministic online algorithms. Moreover, we establish a lower bound of $e$ on the competitive ratio of randomized online algorithms, improving the previous lower bound of $e/(e-1)$. Besides, we combine the two ideas and obtain a randomized learning-augmented algorithm that is $(e/λ+1/λ^2)$-robust and $(e+λ)$-consistent. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to complement our theoretical analysis and evaluate the empirical performance of our algorithms.

</details>


### [248] [MARS-RA: Rank Aggregation for Credit Assignment via Multimodal Comparisons in Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27967)
*Dawei Wang,Di Zhao,Xinyuan Liu,Marci Chi Ma,Xiaoyang Liu,Chengming Zhou,Gary Ushaw,Richard Davison*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MARS-RA框架，将多智能体强化学习中的信用分配问题重构为基于大模型 pairwise 比较的排序聚合问题，并通过潜在奖励塑形提升协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具身AI中奖励稀疏、延迟及智能体数量动态变化导致的信用分配难题，传统绝对估计方法对噪声和动态参与敏感。

Method: 利用多模态大模型生成贡献度两两比较，转化为排序聚合问题，采用相对估计替代绝对估计，将比较结果转为贡献分数用于基于势能的奖励塑形，并提供收敛性与鲁棒性理论保证，Shapley值作为解释参考。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的任务上，MARS-RA能有效引导智能体实现高效协作。

Conclusion: 相对排序的信用分配方法在动态和噪声环境下具有优势，为多智能体协作学习提供了稳健且可解释的新途径。

Abstract: Credit assignment is a fundamental challenge in cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, particularly in embodied AI settings characterized by limited and delayed feedback as well as dynamically changing numbers of active agents. We propose MARS-RA, a framework that reformulates credit assignment as a rank aggregation problem using contribution-based pairwise comparisons among agents generated by large multimodal models. This shift from absolute to relative estimation ensures robustness against noise and dynamic agent participation, converting comparison results into contribution scores for potential-based reward shaping. We provide theoretical justification for the convergence and robustness of the proposed framework, and show that Shapley values can be used as an interpretive reference. Experimental results on challenging tasks of different types indicate that MARS-RA can guide agents toward effective cooperation.

</details>


### [249] [TriShield: Zero-Utility-Loss Defense Against Privacy Backdoors in Federated Language Model Fine-Tuning via Orthogonal Gradient Projection and Optimizer State Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27940)
*Cheng Wei*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出TriShield三层防御机制，在不损失模型效用和增加通信轮次的情况下，将NeuroImprint攻击的客户端数据重建率降至0%，且额外GPU计算开销小于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦微调防御（如本地差分隐私、梯度裁剪）无法有效抵御NeuroImprint这类恶意参数服务器发起的隐私后门攻击，且会带来不可接受的效用损失。

Method: 三层确定性防御：参数工件检测器识别内存神经元签名；有状态虚拟迭代破坏Adam/AdamW动量状态的闭式求逆；零效用正交投影将梯度投影到主任务语义子空间以消除私有记忆分量。

Result: 在GPT-2（117M）和Llama-Guard-3-1B上，NeuroImprint所有变体的重建率降至0%，训练精度保持或提升，额外GPU计算开销低于5%，且理论上证明上传梯度与单个样本的互信息为零。

Conclusion: TriShield在保持模型效用和通信效率的同时彻底阻断NeuroImprint式重建，为联邦微调提供了一种实用且可证明安全的隐私防护方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) enables collaborative training without exposing raw data. However, a recent attack, NeuroImprint [1] (arXiv:2606.20553), demonstrates that a malicious parameter server can corrupt a PEFT adapter into a privacy backdoor: by assigning a dedicated memorization neuron to each training sample and ensuring each neuron updates at most once, the server can analytically reconstruct 59\%--79\% of client training data with high semantic fidelity. Existing defenses---including local differential privacy (LDP) [8] and gradient clipping---either fail against this attack or impose unacceptable utility degradation. We present \textbf{TriShield}, a three-layer deterministic defense that completely prevents NeuroImprint-style reconstruction with \textbf{zero model utility loss} and \textbf{no additional communication rounds}. TriShield consists of: (1) a \textbf{Parameter Artifact Detector} that identifies memory-neuron signatures in distributed model parameters before local training begins; (2) a \textbf{Stateful Virtual Iteration} mechanism that forces Adam/AdamW's momentum state to irreversibly entangle gradients across virtual steps, invalidating NeuroImprint's closed-form inversion; and (3) a \textbf{Zero-Utility Orthogonal Projection} operator that projects all local gradient updates onto the main-task semantic subspace computed via SVD, physically eliminating any gradient components that carry private memorization. We prove theoretically that after Layers 2 and 3, the mutual information between the uploaded gradient and any individual training sample is zero. Experiments on GPT-2 (117M) and Llama-Guard-3-1B verify that TriShield reduces NeuroImprint reconstruction rate to \textbf{0\%} across all tested attack variants, while maintaining or improving training accuracy, with less than 5\% additional GPU computation overhead.

</details>


### [250] [Generalization Bounds on Optimal Control for Transformer Training and Wasserstein Distributional Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27975)
*Kağan Akman,Naci Saldi,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文推导了在动态规划递归下训练的Transformer的有限样本泛化界，通过将训练问题视为马尔可夫控制问题，并利用量化模型和集中不等式得到显式界，最后联系到Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer泛化分析多依赖静态或简化假设，缺乏对训练中动态规划递归结构的显式刻画，本文旨在从控制论和测度值视角提供更严格且可转移的有限样本保证。

Method: 利用双重提升的测度值Transformer动力学，将数据集建模为经验输入-输出测度对的概率定律，把训练转化为有限时域马尔可夫控制问题；对状态、动作和测度状态空间进行量化，通过有限度量空间上经验定律的集中不等式与值函数的Lipschitz稳定性导出量化模型的有限样本界，再以显式逼近误差转回原模型。

Result: 获得了量化模型的显式有限样本泛化界，并给出将其迁移至原模型的近似误差代价；同时将相同机制推广至分布鲁棒控制，建立Transformer泛化与Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化的联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为Transformer在动态规划训练下的泛化提供了一种基于控制论和量化分析的理论框架，并揭示了其与分布鲁棒优化在测度值表述下的内在关联。

Abstract: We derive finite-sample generalization bounds for Transformers trained with dynamic programming recursions. Building on the doubly lifted, measure-valued formulation of Transformer dynamics, we view data sets as probability laws on pairs of empirical input-output measures, allowing us to interpret the training problem as a finite-horizon Markovian control problem. We then analyze a quantized model, derived by quantizing the state, action, and measure-state spaces, and derive explicit finite-sample generalization bounds using concentration inequalities for empirical laws on finite metric spaces together with a Lipschitz stability estimate for the value function. These bounds are transferred to the base model at the cost of an explicit approximation error. Finally, we show that the same machinery yields a distributionally robust control formulation of the training problem, connecting Transformer generalization to Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization.

</details>


### [251] [MIND: Lightweight and Effective Memory Injection Defense for LLM Agents via Intent-Aware Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28103)
*Dongyi Liu,Haixing He,Xiaobao Wu,Jia Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MIND，一种轻量级防御框架，利用意图感知信息瓶颈提取关键表征，检测并抵御记忆注入攻击，在降低攻击成功率的同时保持任务准确性和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法要么计算成本高，要么在多轮上下文中存在信息冗余，难以高效应对记忆注入攻击。

Method: 基于良性轨迹与恶意轨迹在初始用户意图与后续行为间存在可区分关系的观察，MIND使用意图感知信息瓶颈从初始意图和逐轮行为中提取紧凑的意图-行为表征，过滤任务无关和重复信息，再由轻量级检测器识别恶意记忆。

Result: 在ReAct-StrategyQA上，平均ASR-r和ASR-a分别降低55.4%和55.3%，且平均准确率和延迟与未防御智能体相当。

Conclusion: MIND在降低攻击成功率的同时保持任务性能和推理效率，是一种有效且实用的记忆注入攻击防御方案。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM-based agents are vulnerable to memory injection attacks: Agents may retrieve poisoned memory from attackers, which diverts their behavior from initial user intent and finally causes task failure. However, existing defense mechanisms either incur high computational cost or suffer from information redundancy in multi-turn contexts. To address these challenges, we propose Memory Intent-Aware Neural Denoising(MIND), a lightweight defense framework for memory injection attack. Our preliminary analysis reveals that benign and poisoned trajectories exhibit distinguishable relationships between the initial user intent and subsequent behavior. Building on this observation, MIND employs an intent-aware Information Bottleneck(IB) to extract compact intent--behavior representations from the initial intent and turn-level behavior. The IB preserves intent-relevant cross-turn attack signals while filtering task-irrelevant and repetitive information, and a lightweight detector identifies malicious memories from the resulting representations. As such, MIND mitigates information redundancy in multi-turn contexts while avoiding the overhead of repeated LLM auditing. Extensive experiments show that MIND reduces attack success rates while preserving task accuracy and inference efficiency. Notably, on ReAct-StrategyQA, MIND reduces mean ASR-r and ASR-a by 55.4% and 55.3%, respectively, while matching the undefended agent in average accuracy and latency.

</details>


### [252] [Chem World: A Large-Scale Benchmark and Physics-Informed Framework for Trustworthy Chemical Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28079)
*Tianyou Bai,Huan Wang,Mingchen Gao,Fangyue Lin,Pinze Ren,Zhenlin Zhao,Siming Dong*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了一个集成17个数据集、超80万分子样本的化学性质预测基准Chem World，以及融合化学先验知识的物理信息神经网络Mixture-PINN，证明其在准确性和鲁棒性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学性质预测基准存在任务多样性不足、数据集碎片化、评估协议不一致的问题，难以系统评估AI模型的可靠性和泛化能力。

Method: 构建统一基准Chem World，覆盖密度、电导率、溶解度等多种性质的17个数据集；提出Mixture-PINN，将化学先验知识嵌入数据驱动学习，以提升预测精度和可靠性。

Result: 在Chem World上的大量实验表明，Mixture-PINN相比现有方法在准确性和鲁棒性上更优。

Conclusion: Chem World结合大规模标准化评估和物理信息学习，为计算化学中可信AI系统的开发奠定了基础，并推动AI驱动的科学发现。

Abstract: Chemical property prediction plays a critical role in accelerating scientific discovery in chemistry, materials science, and drug development. However, existing benchmarks often suffer from limited task diversity, fragmented datasets, and inconsistent evaluation protocols, making it challenging to systematically assess the reliability and generalization of AI models. In this work, we introduce Chem World, a comprehensive benchmark for chemical property prediction that integrates 17 diverse chemical datasets with over 800,000 molecular samples, covering various properties including density, electrical conductivity, solubility, and other molecular characteristics. Chem World provides a unified platform for evaluating AI models across multiple property prediction tasks. Furthermore, we propose Mixture-PINN, a physics-informed neural network based prediction framework that incorporates chemical prior knowledge into data-driven learning, improving the accuracy, robustness, and reliability of chemical property prediction. Extensive experiments on Chem World demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach compared with existing methods. By combining large-scale standardized evaluation with physics-informed learning, Chem World establishes a foundation for developing trustworthy AI systems for computational chemistry and advancing AI-driven scientific discovery.

</details>


### [253] [CDAE: Enhancing Perturbation Robustness in Pretrained Language Models with Contrastive Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28236)
*Sina Heydari,Amirreza Abbasi,Mohsen Hooshmand,Majid Ramezani*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出轻量级对比去噪自编码器（CDAE），联合优化对比学习和重构目标，提升BERT句子嵌入对同义替换、掩码等扰动的鲁棒性，稳定性优于BERT和SimCSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型的句子嵌入对语义保持型文本扰动（如同义词替换、掩码、词丢弃）敏感，导致表示不稳定，现有方法未充分解决该问题。

Method: CDAE基于BERT，引入对比学习拉近扰动样本与原始样本的表示，同时通过重构目标保留语义信息，实现扰动不变表示学习。

Result: 在多种扰动策略和强度下，CDAE的嵌入相似度一致性优于原始BERT和SimCSE，且扰动越强优势越明显，同时保持语义信息。

Conclusion: 扰动不变学习是提升句子嵌入稳定性的有效方向，CDAE提供了轻量且可复现的解决方案，代码已公开。

Abstract: Pre-trained language models have significantly improved sentence representation learning, yet their embedding remain sensitive to semantic preserving textual perturbations such as synonym substitution, masking and word dropout. This work proposes a lightweight Contrastive Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE) that refines pre-trained BERT embedding by jointly optimizing contrastive and reconstruction objective to learn perturbation-invariant representation. We evaluate the proposed framework using multiple perturbation strategies with varying strengths and compare it against the original BERT embeddings and SimCSE. Experimental results show that CDAE consistently preserves higher embedding similarity under perturbations, with the improvements becoming more pronounced as framework effectively enhances representation stability while preserving semantic information, highlighting perturbation-invariant learning as a promising direction for improving sentence embeddings. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/ComputationIASBS/CDAE

</details>


### [254] [Semi-Supervised Learning for Molecular Graphs via Ensemble Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28304)
*Rasmus Tirsgaard,Laurits Fredsgaard,Marisa Wodrich,Mikkel Jordahn,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出基于集成共识的半监督学习方法，利用无标签分子数据提升图神经网络在分子性质预测中的准确率、鲁棒性和校准性，且单个模型性能优于传统全集成监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子科学中标注数据昂贵且耗时，而无标签分子数据丰富；传统半监督方法依赖保标签增强，在分子领域难以设计，因为微小变化可能剧烈改变性质。

Method: 采用集成共识作为半监督训练目标，不依赖数据增强，通过多个模型对无标签数据预测的一致性来引导单个模型的学习，类似知识蒸馏机制。

Result: 在多种分子数据集、任务类型和图神经网络架构下，该方法提升预测精度；单个集成成员表现优于传统监督训练的全集成，并降低校准误差。

Conclusion: 集成共识式半监督训练是分子性质预测的有效策略，兼具高精度、鲁棒性和良好校准性，且计算效率优于传统集成方法。

Abstract: Machine learning is transforming molecular sciences by accelerating property prediction, simulation, and the discovery of new molecules and materials. Acquiring labeled data in these domains is often costly and time-consuming, whereas large collections of unlabeled molecular data are readily available. Standard semi-supervised learning methods often rely on label-preserving augmentations, which are challenging to design in the molecular domain, where minor changes can drastically alter properties. In this work, we show that semi-supervised methods that rely on an ensemble consensus can boost predictive accuracy across a diverse range of molecular datasets, task types, and graph neural network architectures. We find that training with an ensemble consensus objective increases robustness in models and exhibits an effect similar to knowledge distillation; an individual member of an ensemble trained this way outperforms a full ensemble trained in a traditional supervised fashion in almost all cases. In addition, this type of semi-supervised training reduces calibration error.

</details>


### [255] [MemHarness: Memory Is Reconstructed, Not Replayed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28272)
*Rong Wu,Daocheng Fu,Licheng Wen,Xuemeng Yang,Shu Zou,Jianbiao Mei,Yuxin Wang,Hairong Zhang,Yu Yang,Tao Hu,Cong Zhang,Botian Shi,Pinlong Cai*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MemHarness框架，让LLM代理基于当前情境主动重构检索到的历史经验，而非机械重放，显著提升决策性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强代理将检索经验视为静态记录直接注入上下文，忽视经验抽象性与当前状态具体性之间的差距，常导致负迁移；人类则倾向于重组记忆以适应新情境。

Method: 在每个决策步骤，使用统一策略模型（经GRPO端到端训练）批判并重构检索到的经验，生成情境相关的指导后再行动，使重构能力自然涌现。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，MemHarness显著优于纯RL和静态记忆增强基线，并在分布外场景中表现出强鲁棒性；重构目标同时防止负迁移并作为训练时的潜在引导。

Conclusion: 基于情境的经验重构能从根本上提升代理的内在推理能力，是超越静态记忆重放的有效范式。

Abstract: Retrieving past experiences has become a common strategy to enhance large language model agents. However, most existing memory-augmented agents treat retrieved experiences as static records to be replayed verbatim, injecting them into the context regardless of whether they align with the agent's current situation. This ``replay'' paradigm ignores the gap between the abstract, general nature of stored experience and the concrete, ever-changing states encountered at decision time, frequently causing negative transfer. In contrast, humans rarely recall past experiences verbatim; instead, they reorganize and adapt retrieved memories to fit the present context. Inspired by this, we propose MemHarness, a framework that equips LLM agents to actively harness and reconstruct past experiences based on the present context. At each decision step, a unified policy model critiques and reconstructs the retrieved experience conditioned on the current state, producing context-grounded guidance before acting. This reconstructive ability emerges naturally through end-to-end training with GRPO. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that MemHarness substantially outperforms pure RL and static memory-augmented baselines, demonstrating strong robustness in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Furthermore, our analyses reveal that this reconstruction objective not only prevents negative transfer but also serves as latent guidance during training, fundamentally improving the agent's intrinsic reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [256] [One Human, $N$ Agents: Audit-Budget Allocation for LLM Agent Fleets under Miscalibrated, Correlated Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28317)
*Cesare Zavattari,Alessandro Tommasi,Giuseppe Prencipe*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 在预算远小于智能体数量的审计场景下，本文基于两层高斯copula模型定位了对抗性置信度误校准阈值δ*，发现当误校准超过该阈值时按置信度排序审计反而劣于随机审计，并证明预算越小δ*越高、跨家族相关性由共享难度主导而非血缘关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中对大量LLM代理的审计预算极其有限，且模型自报置信度可能被对抗性误校准并存在相关性误差，导致现有按置信度优先的审计策略可能失效，甚至不如随机选择，因此需要理论刻画这种反转发生的条件。

Method: 将预算化噪声检查建模为两层高斯copula，推导置信度排序审计与随机审计的期望效用差，求解误校准阈值δ*；利用五个开源模型与一个专有模型的实测置信度分布进行点估计与置信区间分析，并在真实记录轨迹上回放策略验证排序。

Result: 开源模型置信度近似常数（操作上无信息），点估计位于或超过翻转阈值但置信区间跨阈值；专有模型置信度有信息且位于阈值之下；预算缩小时δ*不降反升，跨家族相关性并非低而是共享难度主导。

Conclusion: 给出了“空洞监督”的定量判据，即当模型置信度误校准超过δ*时审计策略形同虚设，应改用随机审计或重新校准；实际模型差异显著，提示只依赖自报置信度不可靠，需结合外部信号设计鲁棒审计机制。

Abstract: A single human must audit $N$ LLM agents under a budget of $B \ll N$ audits per round, guided by self-reported confidence that may be adversarially miscalibrated and by correlated errors. We model this as budgeted noisy inspection over a two-level Gaussian copula and locate the miscalibration threshold $δ^*$ past which confidence-ranked auditing is \emph{worse} than random. Two a-priori expectations reverse: $δ^*$ \emph{rises} as the budget shrinks, and cross-family correlation is not low---shared difficulty dominates lineage. Five open-weight LLMs show operationally useless (near-constant) confidence, point estimates at or beyond the flip though CIs straddle it; a proprietary model is informative and lands below it. We give a quantitative criterion for \emph{vacuous} oversight, and replaying policies on recorded traces confirms the ordering.

</details>


### [257] [Hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo for Order-Optimal Neural Actor-Critic in Average-Reward CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28390)
*Ankur Naskar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）的神经评论家估计器，解决平均奖励CMDP中神经评论家的偏差-成本权衡，实现首阶最优收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原始-对偶演员-评论家方法在线性评论家下已有最优收敛理论，但神经评论家在NTK分析下存在偏差与优化成本的根本矛盾，导致无法达到阶最优收敛。

Method: 引入分层MLMC神经评论家，同时在轨迹采样和评论家优化两个维度进行去偏，使估计器在获得长运行偏差的同时仅需对数级样本成本；并据此设计原始-对偶自然演员-评论家算法。

Result: 在无限时域平均奖励CMDP中，对一般策略参数化和神经评论家实现最优性差距与约束违反均达到~O(T^{-1/2})，且无需已知混合时间；在无约束情形下结果同样新颖。

Conclusion: 首次在神经评论家设定下为平均奖励CMDP提供阶最优收敛保证，消除混合时间已知假设，拓展了安全强化学习的理论边界。

Abstract: Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) provide a natural framework for reinforcement learning in safety-critical applications, where agents maximize long-term reward while satisfying long-term constraints. Although primal-dual actor-critic methods with linear critics are well understood, extending order-optimal convergence guarantees to neural critics in average-reward CMDPs has remained open. The main challenge is a fundamental bias-cost trade-off in neural critic estimation: under Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) analysis, reducing critic bias substantially increases critic optimization cost, preventing order-optimal convergence in the primal-dual framework. We resolve this bottleneck by introducing a hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) neural critic that performs debiasing simultaneously across trajectory sampling and critic optimization. The resulting estimator attains the bias of a long critic optimization run with only logarithmic expected sample cost. Building on this estimator, we develop a primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm that achieves both an optimality gap and a constraint violation of order $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$.
